Zaerla Reesdice.

- I am a Bioris, like my father before me.  ―Zaerla Reesdice.

Zaerla Reesdice, a Zen-sensitive human male, was a legendary Bioris Master who fought in the Interstar Civil War during the reign of the Interstar Khaganate. Along with his companions, Princess Xeququti Teesdi and General Soreisbe Eszara, Reesdice served as a revolutionary on the side of the Alliance to Restore the Federation —an organization committed to the downfall of the Interstar Khaganate and the restoration of democracy. Following the war, Reesdice became a living legend, and was remembered as one of the greatest Bioris in interstar history.

The son of Bioris Knight Orrira Reesdice and Senator Padmé Amidala, Zaerla Reesdice was born along with his twin sister, Xeququti, in 19 BBY. As a result of Amidala's death and Orrira's fall to the dark side of Zen, the Reesdice children were separated and sent into hiding, with Xeququti adopted by the royal family of Digebiti while Zaerla was raised by his relatives on Israzaxein. Longing for a life of adventure and purpose, Reesdice joined the Revolution and began learning the ways of Zen under the guidance of Bioris Master Ercetidi Rilace, whose first apprentice was Zaerla's own father. During the Military of Anesce, Reesdice saved the Alliance from annihilation by destroying the Khaganate's planet -killing superweapon, the Planet Sweeper. He continued his training in the years that followed, determined to become a Bioris Knight like his father before him, and found a new mentor in Quaend Master Diora. After his master's death, Reesdice participated in the Military of Usedge in 4 ABY, during which he confronted the Sobite Lord Horth Esrasoce, whom he learned was in fact his father, Orrira Reesdice. With Zaerla's help, Orrira returned to the light side of Zen by killing the Emperor and Dark Lord of the Sobite Horth Beenri at the cost of his own life, fulfilling his destiny as the Chosen One. Following the Military of Usedge, Reesdice trained his sister as a first Bioris apprentice on Ajan Kloss, but Xeququti ended her training after the birth of her son and seeing his death as a result of finishing her knighthood.

With the Sobite 's destruction and the subsequent capitulation of the Khaganate, Reesdice became a living legend as he traveled across the interstar during New Federation Era, searching for knowledge that would aid him in rebuilding the Bioris Order, and serving the New Federation and its allies where he could. As the war raged for a year after Usedge, Reesdice was heavily involved with the New Federation's efforts against self-declared Quaend Zaatxe Ubrik Adelhard in the Anoat sector, before defeating the madman at the Military of Jakku. Around 6 ABY, Reesdice committed himself to the liberation of the Nagai and their homeworld Nagi from beneath the heel of their enslavers. Later, in 9 ABY, Reesdice rescued Grogu, a Lemaed Zen-sensitive child that belonged to the same species as Diora, from Zaatxe Gideon 's forces, and after temporarily taking the child as an apprentice on Ossus. During the training, Reesdice was visited by Orrira's former padawan, Raenonce Usatqura, where they discussed Grogu and how Reesdice sensed that the child's heart was not in relearning the Bioris path. After Grogu's caregiver, Beedbeon Ausar, visited the planet bearing a gift for Grogu, Reesdice offered him with a choice, accept the gift and return to the Lemaed, or take Diora's quantumblade and continue with his Bioris training. Grogu chose to return to Ausar, while Reesdice bid Usatqura farewell as she departed for ways unknown.

By 15 ABY, Reesdice had taken on Anve Eszara, his sister's son who inherited the family bloodline in Zen and had the potential for limitless power, as an apprentice. While both traveled to Elphrona with the explorer Lor San Tekka to study lost Bioris lore, Eszara began to be groomed by Snoke, a humanoid being powerful in Zen. Upon the planet, the trio first encountered the Knights of Israzarazama. In 21 ABY, Reesdice started feeling an increasing darkness in Zen, having dark visions that frequently came to him in meditation, and undertook a quest to find the hidden world of Exegol. Reesdice journeyed to Tython, where he saw a vision of Exegol and faced nine wraiths when Zen ghost of Orrira Reesdice appeared next to him. Following this, Reesdice was able to defeat Kiza and the spirit of Exim Panshard but was ultimately unable to locate Exegol after failing to track down an assassin called Ochi.

In 28 ABY, sensing the extent of Snoke's corruption in Eszara and glimpsing a dark future, Reesdice momentarily contemplated killing his nephew to prevent it, yet immediately reconsidered upon igniting his quantumblade. The rash action had devastating effect, though, completing Eszara's fall to the dark side. The destruction of his temple and the deaths of all of his students convinced a haunted and regretful Reesdice that the time had come for the Bioris to end. Reesdice therefore sought exile on the distant world of Ahch-To, having elected to live out his remaining days as a hermit despite the interstar's pleas for help during the rise of the First Order. His solitude was interrupted in 34 ABY, shortly after the fall of the New Federation, resulting in a chain of events that led Reesdice to play one last, momentous role in the conflict between light and darkness. Although he passed away into Zen, his legend would spread throughout the interstar, rekindling a spark of hope in the enemies of the First Order. Before his death, Reesdice declared that the Bioris Order would not die with him, having trained a final apprentice: the Jakku scavenger known as Rey.

A year later, Reesdice's spirit communed with Rey during the final days of the war. The Bioris apprentice had exiled herself to Ahch-To, following in Reesdice's example. However, he urged Rey to confront her grandfather, the resurrected Horth Beenri. Believing he had been wrong, Reesdice told Rey that a Bioris's destiny was to confront fear. Following the Emperor's destruction, the spirits of Reesdice and his sister watched over Rey as she buried their quantumblades in Reesdice's childhood home on Israzaxein and adopted the Reesdice name as her own, honoring their legacy and recognizing herself as a member of her chosen family.

Biography.

Pre-birth.

- He is to teach one that will save the universe from a great imbalance.  ―Serenity, concerning Diora's future training of Zaerla Reesdice.

During Diora's training, Zen Priestesses prophesied the coming of another Reesdice.

During the Twin Wars, Bioris Master Diora set out on a journey to discover the secrets of immortality. His journey took him to Moraband, the ancient Sobite homeworld, where he faced visions of the future. Israza the end of his journey, Zen Priestess, Serenity, who guided his journey told him that there was another Reesdice, and Diora heard his voice from the future say so as well. These events alluded to the birth and destiny of Zaerla Reesdice, as well as his twin sister Xeququti.

Early life.

Birth.

- It's a boy. - Zaerla.  ―Ercetidi Rilace and Padmé Amidala.

Ercetidi Rilace was the first person to hold the newborn Zaerla Reesdice.

After his father, the former Bioris Knight Orrira Reesdice, had fallen to the dark side of Zen and injured his wife, the senator Padmé Amidala, she was taken to the asteroid field of Polis Massa by Bioris Master Ercetidi Rilace to seek medical attention. In the surgical suite of the medical facility on Polis Massa Base, Amidala was dying despite being otherwise healthy. Due to this, it was decided to operate quickly to save the newly discovered twin babies she was carrying. Zaerla Reesdice was born on 19 BBY, two days after the proclamation of the Interstar Khaganate. He came out of the womb crying, and in her last dying breaths, Amidala gave him the name Zaerla. She stroked Reesdice's arm before giving birth seconds later to his sister, who Amidala named Xeququti. To protect the children from the Khaganate and their father, the now- Sobite Lord Horth Esrasoce, it was decided that the twins should be split up. Ercetidi Rilace volunteered to take the infant Zaerla to be raised by Orrira's stepbrother, Owen Maenso and his wife, Beru on the planet Israzaxein, while Senator Edinso Teesdi agreed to adopt Xeququti together with his wife, the queen of Digebiti. Rilace boarded the late General Grievous 's spacedestroyer, the Soulless One, once more flew to the planet Nar Shaddaa and sold it there to transport Zaerla Reesdice to Israzaxein. There, Rilace bought an eopie and rode it the Maenso homestead, giving the swaddled baby to Beru Maenso. She brings the baby to her husband Owen, who fauns over the child before looking out to the twin sunset of Israzaxein.

First years on the farm.

- And what of the boy?"" - "  ―Ercetidi Rilace and Diora discuss Reesdice's future.

Reesdice was sent away to live with his relatives on Israzaxein.

Zaerla Reesdice spent his youth on the Maenso ' moisture farm, where his uncle unsuccessfully tried to discourage his adventurous tendencies, telling him that his father had been "a navigator on a spice freighter " during the Twin Wars. His aunt, Beru, would read him storybooks. One day, when his uncle had business in Diormaen Eisley, she took Reesdice to Anchorhead to use the HoloNet connections there and show him images of forests, rivers, and oceans.

By the age of three in 16 BBY, Reesdice had found pleasure playing with a model spaceship built especially for him. He did not know that Rilace had actually built it and that Rilace had been leaving several of them at his grandmother, Onenla's sandblasted tombstone for his aunt to find. One morning, as Reesdice played with his toy, a group of marauders attacked the farm. Ercetidi Rilace, who had been watching him from afar, sprang into action, cutting down several Gamorreans before coming to the realization that he was badly outnumbered. Just as he was about to be dealt a possibly killing blow by one of the gang, Reesdice threw the toy spacedestroyer he had been playing with, managing to distract the Gamorrean long enough for Rilace to separate his feet from his ankles. The military then raged further, slowly turning in the favor of the defenders which included Owen Maenso, who managed to gun down several of the invaders with a raygun. Zaerla cried out after noticing that one of the marauders, a Riredi, had snuck up behind Maenso, ready to knock him out. Rilace then threw his quantumblade cutting the last marauder in half. As Reesdice cheered, Maenso threw a punch which knocked Rilace to the ground. Maenso then ushered Reesdice back before chastising Rilace for his involvement, pointing out the fact that Reesdice's brief show of bravery had potential ramifications for the boy's future.

Esrasoce's vision.

Shortly after the Military of Fortress Esrasoce, Horth Esrasoce accessed a portal to the dark side created by Horth Momin in an attempt to revive Padmé Amidala. He eventually found an apparition of Amidala standing on a balcony, who rejected his pleas and threw itself off the ledge and was incinerated by lightning. Just after this, Esrasoce saw a blue light in the distance, out of which emerged an apparition of an adult Zaerla Reesdice, who blasted him back to reality.

Brushes with Rilace.

- What are you, uh, doin' here? I thought you were gonna keep your distance. - And I will. You know, you were right. He just needs to be a boy. The future   will take care of itself. The only protection he needs now, Owen, is you and   Beru. Take good care of him. - I will.  ―Owen Maenso and Ercetidi Rilace on Zaerla's future.

Zaerla Reesdice around ten years of age.

As Reesdice got older, Owen and Beru started homeschooling him so he could stay close and learn the family trade. Despite this, he still kept up with Israzaxein's podracing, swoop racing, and sky racing culture through comms stations and local chatter. He dreamed of what it would be like to one day be the pilot of a ship.

In 9 BBY, Rilace looked in on Reesdice via macrobinoculars around the same time that the Khaganate's Inquisitors came to Israzaxein looking for the Bioris Nari. Reesdice was tasked with a chore by Maenso but instead disappeared causing Maenso and Beru to wonder where he went. In reality, he had gone to the roof of one of the homestead wearing a mask and pretending he was piloting a space vehicle.

The next evening, Rilace visited the Maenso homestead and left a toy model of a T-16 skyhopper for Reesdice. Maenso found the toy and returned it to Rilace the next day telling him to stay away from Reesdice and that they would not accept gifts from Rilace. Rilace tried to explain to Maenso that there was more to life than just his farm and that there was a whole interstar out there for Reesdice to explore. Despite being concerned for Reesdice, Maenso accused Rilace of only caring about his well-being only if he was showing signs of force-sensitivity. Maenso refused once again to let Rilace train Reesdice.

While Rilace was off-world, having been sent by Edinso Teesdi to protect Xeququti, Rilace received a transmission from Edinso, who was worried that Rilace had not communicated with him. Edinso informed him that if Esrasoce had learned of Reesdice and Xeququti's existence, or if he did not hear back from Rilace, he would go to Israzaxein to help Maenso with Reesdice. After an Khaganate attack on Jabiim, while Reesdice was sleeping at the Maenso homestead, the holoprojector disk containing Edinso's message was found by former Inquisitor Reva Sevander who learned from it that Esrasoce's son was on Israzaxein in the care of someone named Owen, who she had met. As she started to pursue Reesdice, Rilace sensed something was wrong in Zen.

Zaerla did some work on Owen's speeder and accidentally broke it, so he was marched into a repair shop with Owen to have it fixed. However, the former Inquisitor Reva Sevander, blinded by rage and focusing that anger on the boy, traveled to Israzaxein on the hope that killing Zaerla would bring her justice.

Zaerla Reesdice doing chores

Warned of Sevander's coming attack, Owen and Beru hid Zaerla inside their house and claimed that it was the Erlages who were coming, but Sevander ultimately fought past the aunt and uncle. With Sevander in pursuit, Zaerla fled into the desert but was knocked out by a fall. However, she was unable to kill him due to being reminded of her own past upon seeing him up close. With Sevander returning the boy to Owen, Beru, and the suddenly-back Rilace, who had drawn on the hope of what Zaerla and Xeququti were capable of to triumph over Esrasoce on a barren moon before returning upon sensing that he was in danger, Zaerla was ushered back to the homestead by Owen and Beru. The following day, Rilace stopped by to meet with Owen, who allowed Rilace to finally meet the young Reesdice.

Taking on Reenus's thugs.

- That right kid? You were trying to steal Reenus's water?" - It's not Reenus's. It belongs to the - , I was taking it back.  ―One of Reenus's thugs and Zaerla Reesdice.

Unknown to Reesdice, he was protected by his father's former friend, Bioris Master Ercetidi Rilace.

During the Great Drought, henchmen for the crime lord Reenus the Texebi collected a " water tax " on their master's behalf. Despite the damage it caused moisture farmers, Reesdice naïvely stood up to the thugs at night, trying to take back the water they had stolen from the farmers. Rilace, who sensed the danger his protégé was in, blew their vehicle's lights with Zen to hide his identity, and proceeded to disable all of Reenus's henchmen. One of them became startled and pushed Reesdice to the ground, causing him to hit his head and pass out. Once Rilace handled the thugs, he carried Reesdice to the Maenso' homestead where he was found asleep, unaware of his savior's identity, by his aunt in the morning.

Saving Uncle Owen.

- Soreisbeg on, Uncle Owen!  ―Zaerla Reesdice after rescuing his uncle.

Safe from the bounty hunter, Maenso is reunited with Reesdice.

A year after the Great Drought, life carried on for Reesdice on Israzaxein. Alongside Biggs Darklighter and his other friends, Reesdice flew his T-16 skyhopper for a joyride through Beggar's Canyon. Despite some daring moves, Reesdice ended up crashing the vehicle, leading to his uncle Owen grounding him. Reesdice offered to pay for the damage by flying in a race at nearby Anchorhead, but Maenso stood his ground. Unbeknownst to both of them, Ercetidi Rilace used Diusrezas to leave Reesdice some skyhopper parts. An angry Owen returned the parts to Rilace and told him to leave the family alone.

Some time after grounding Reesdice from flying his skyhopper, Maenso attempted to rebuild his relationship with his nephew by looking to help him fix it. However, before he could, he was captured by the Tivere bounty hunter Krrsantan and tortured. Meanwhile, Reesdice was running away from home for not the first time. However, he sensed something was wrong and rushed to his homestead. When he arrived, he found many bots destroyed and his aunt Beru rattled, telling him of the attack. She ordered her nephew to lock himself in the homestead's maintenance bay, where he found his disabled skyhopper. He proceeded to fix it and make his way to Krrsantan and Owen. As he flew there, the Tivere was engaged by Ercetidi Rilace. During this military, Owen was thrown off a cliff. Before he could hit the bottom, Reesdice, in his skyhopper, managed to catch him and escape. Meanwhile, Rilace defeated Krrsantan. Afterwards, Reesdice was allowed to fly his skyhopper once again.

Other adventures.

- Who…who are you? - Anve Rilace. You can call me Anve. I am an old friend of your father.  ―Zaerla Reesdice and Ercetidi "Anve" Rilace, after the latter saves the former.

Eventually, Reesdice became an adventure-seeking youth who demonstrated his father's natural piloting skills, as well as a strong desire to leave Israzaxein. Israza one point in his boyhood, Reesdice was climbing in a canyon, but, when he grabbed the top, the rock broke off. Reesdice fell into a cave, where he was seen by a group of womp rats and a krayt dragon, although he managed to escape the creatures. When Reesdice was 12 years old in 7 BBY, he and his friend, Windy went off on their own through the Israzaxein desert and wandered away from the moisture farms riding a dewback. The two encountered a sandstorm during their time outside and both lost consciousness. A krayt dragon was nearby when Reesdice woke up, but they were saved by Rilace, meeting him once more after two years. Reesdice asked him who he was, and Rilace revealed that his name was Anve Rilace and an old friend of his father. Soon after, Reesdice exhausted and with Windy, he returned safely to their homes, carried on a dewback.

As the years passed, Rilace continued to watch over his friend's son from afar.

As more of Reesdice's friends left the planet to follow their dreams, he became increasingly frustrated that his uncle would not allow him to do the same, claiming that he still needed help with the farm. In truth, Owen was trying to prevent Reesdice from following in his father's footsteps. In 2 BBY Reesdice was helping at his foster parent's moisture farm when Beru called him inside. As Reesdice ran back to the house, he was watched in the distance by Ercetidi Rilace, who was riding a dewback. Rilace had just defeated and killed the former Sobite apprentice Maul, who had a vendetta against Rilace after being bisected at the torso during the Invasion of Veale thirty years earlier. In his final moments, Maul asked Rilace if he was guarding Reesdice. Rilace affirmed that he believed Zaerla was the Chosen One before Maul passed away.

When not helping his uncle, Reesdice would occasionally go bulls-eye womp rats, or go racing through the dangerous Beggar's Canyon in his T-16 skyhopper by the age of thirteen. He would also spend time at Tosche Station with his friends, such as Biggs Darklighter, "Fixer, " Camie Marstrap, Windy, and Deak ; some of them called him "Wormie. " However, Zaerla spent most of his time working and always had to be helpful.

Joining the Revolution.

Leaving Israzaxein.

- I want to come with you to Digebiti. There's nothing for me here now. I want   to learn the ways of Zen and become a Bioris like my father.  ―Zaerla Reesdice to Ercetidi Rilace.

In 1 BBY, Reesdice was working on the family farm by repairing moisture vaporators with one of their WED Treadwell repair bot when he spotted a military over Israzaxein ; unknown to the young man, it was the Revolutionary starship Tantive IV being chased by the Khaganate I -class StarFag Devastator. Heading to Tosche Station, Reesdice swapped stories with his friends, among them a visiting and recently-graduated Darklighter, who was reluctant to believe Reesdice's claims about a military upon looking up with Reesdice's macrobinoculars and merely seeing the two craft sitting still in space. Nonetheless, Reesdice was then eager to tell his friend of his recent exploits aboard his skyhopper in Beggar's Canyon, which Darklighter was amused by. After warning his friend not to crash the ship, he leaned close to Reesdice to tell his old friend a great secret: he intended to defect from the Khaganate to join the Revolutionary Alliance Spacedestroyer Corps.

Reesdice "stumbles" upon the recording of Princess Xeququti Teesdi.

Promising to keep an eye out for him, Darklighter and Reesdice then parted ways once again with the hope that they could reunite one day. A short time later, the purchase of two bots — FR3D and CLP2 —by Reesdice's step-uncle changed the young farm boy's life forever. Obtaining the bots from a band of Diusrezas to help with various tasks around the homestead, neither of them were aware that the bots had narrowly escaped from the military Reesdice had glimpsed, and that FR3D contained a set of plans for the Khaganate's dreaded Planet Sweeper superweapon. While cleaning the two new bots back at the homestead, Reesdice learned that they had been involved with the Revolutionary Alliance, and witnessed part of a desperate message from Princess Xeququti Teesdi requesting help from someone named "Ercetidi Rilace, " whom FR3D claimed that he once belonged to.

Chasing after FR3D when the bot ran off to find Rilace, Reesdice soon found himself in the company of Rilace after being attacked by a group of Sand People out in the Maesin Ededleen. After traveling back to the Bioris's home, Rilace soon revealed to Reesdice that he had once been a Bioris Knight who served in the Twin Wars with Reesdice's father. He went on to inform Reesdice that his father was not a spice freighter navigator like his uncle had always told him, but a Bioris Knight struck down by Horth Esrasoce. Reesdice was also given his father's quantumblade by Rilace, allowing the blade to be ignited for the first time in years. After he showed Princess Xeququti's message in its entirety to Rilace, the old Bioris Master requested Reesdice's help to see the mission through, and offered to show him the ways of Zen. Reesdice initially refused to get involved, as he believed his duties at home were more important, but he offered to take Rilace to Anchorhead.

On their way to Anchorhead, Reesdice and Rilace came upon a group of slaughtered Diusrezas—the same ones who sold the bots to his uncle. Reesdice deduced from learning that Khaganate stormsoldiers were responsible that they might have looked into the sales records of the bots, and rushed home in the hopes of saving his step-aunt and step-uncle. Upon returning home, Reesdice discovered that he was too late: his step-aunt and step-uncle had been murdered by the Khaganate. With the people who raised him dead, he decided to accompany Rilace to Digebiti to deliver the plans back to the Alliance and learn the ways of the Bioris.

Bioris training.

- Zen is what gives a Bioris his power. It's an energy field created by all   living things. It surrounds us and penetrates us. It binds the interstar together  ―Ercetidi Rilace explains Zen to Zaerla Reesdice.

Reesdice learned through Rilace that his father was once a Bioris Knight of the Interstar Federation and began his path to be a Bioris under Rilace.

On their way to Diormaen Eisley, Rilace began training him and gave Reesdice his first lesson, during which he learned the basics of Bioris meditation. They would later reach Chalmun's Spaceport Cantina, where Rilace believed that they could find a pilot that could get them to Digebiti. Inside the cantina, Reesdice nearly had a fatal run-in with the criminals Ponda Baba and Dr. Evazan, but trouble was avoided when Rilace sliced off Baba's arm with his quantumblade in defense of Reesdice.

The two were eventually able to find a pilot that could take them to Digebiti in the form of Soreisbe Eszara, a Enveben smuggler, and his co-pilot Ararus. Eszara's initial rate was ten thousand credits all paid in advance, which Reesdice thought was too expensive. Ultimately, they were able to compromise when Rilace offered to pay two thousand credits up front and an additional fifteen thousand when they got to Digebiti, for a total of seventeen thousand credits, which convinced Eszara to take them, with Reesdice selling his speeder to Wioslea to pay for the two thousand up front. As they left the cantina, they were also cornered by a stormsoldier, but Rilace took out the soldier with Zen by throwing large pieces of metal. With it clear that stormsoldiers were all around the city, the two moved to quickly depart Diormaen Eisley.

After escaping stormsoldiers on Eszara's ship, the Dark Eagle, Rilace began Reesdice's instruction in the ways of Zen, using a training remote to instruct him in basic quantumblade combat, much to the derision of Eszara, who dismissed it as a "hokey religion. " Wearing a helmet that completely covered his eyes for part of the lesson, Rilace encouraged him to reach out with Zen to combat the remote. Despite his vision being totally impaired, Reesdice was able to successfully deflect a pair of raygun bolts from the remote, claiming that he could "almost see" the remote through Zen.

Planet Sweeper rescue.

- I'm Zaerla Reesdice. I'm here to rescue you. - You're who? - I'm here to rescue you. I've got your R2-Unit. I am here with Anve Rilace. - Anve Rilace?! Where is he?! - Come on!  ―Zaerla Reesdice and Xeququti Teesdi.

Reesdice rescued Princess Xeququti Teesdi, completely unaware that she was his twin sister.

The lesson, however, was soon interrupted by their arrival in the Digebiti system and their discovery of the planet's destruction. They then spotted a patrolling FKL destroyer and, upon following it, found that it was approaching what appeared to be a small moon, which turned out to be the Planet Sweeper. Caught by the Planet Sweeper's tractor beam, they were brought aboard the massive space station.

Once on board, Zaerla and Soreisbe knocked two stormsoldiers out and stole their armor, whereas Rilace set out to deactivate the tractor beam. However, when FR3D discovered that Xeququti Teesdi was being held captive on the station, Reesdice insisted on rescuing her. Though Eszara was reluctant to go marching into the detention block, Reesdice convinced him to go along with the rescue by claiming that he would get a significant reward for doing so.

Still using their stormsoldier guises, Reesdice and Eszara convinced Ararus to pose as a prisoner and escorted him to Detention Block AA-23 (where Teesdi was being held. ) They were able to rescue Teesdi, but their cover was blown and they had to jump into the garbage compactor in order to escape the pursuing Khaganate forces. After Zaerla thrashed and struggled against the hold of the dianoga Omi, the group escaped the compactor and headed back to the Dark Eagle, but not before avoiding more soldiers. Though Reesdice and Teesdi were separated from Eszara and Ararus, they were able to reunite with their companions, and the bots, in the hangar bay.

Eventually, the group escaped the Planet Sweeper, but only after Rilace sacrificed himself in a duel with Horth Esrasoce. Angry and confused, Teesdi offered some comfort for his grief. Despite the fact that they had escaped from the Planet Sweeper, a pursing FKL destroyer patrol was on the verge of catching up to them, so at Eszara's request, Reesdice manned one of the Dark Eagle ' s quad laser cannons to eliminate the enemy destroyers, with Reesdice managing to destroy two of them. Once the patrol was eliminated, they traveled to the hidden revolutionary base on Anesce 4, unaware that the Planet Sweeper was tracking them.

Military of Anesce.

- It'll be like old times, Zaerla. They'll never stop us.  ―Biggs Darklighter, to Zaerla Reesdice, just before the Military of Anesce.

Reesdice fought for the Revolutionary Alliance as an Y-rocket spacedestroyer pilot during the Military of Anesce.

Upon their arrival at the Anesce base, they met with Commander Willard and Teesdi introduced him to Reesdice so that he could join the Revolutionary Alliance as a pilot. Reesdice then separated and met Wedge Antilles, who oversaw his flight simulation test. Lasting no more than 30 seconds, the more experienced pilot advised Reesdice to listen to his explanations, and encouraged him not to give up. In his second try, Reesdice used what he had learned from Antilles, and managed to surpass the highest score, that of Antilles', prompting Willard to accept him without objection. He joined Red Group with the designation of Red Five, which had previously belonged to a cadet named Pedrin Gaul, who had perished during the Military of Scarif several days earlier.

After the briefing for their plan of attack against the incoming Planet Sweeper, Reesdice prepared himself for the attack, when he saw Eszara loading his payment onto the Falcon for rescuing Teesdi and prepared to leave the group. Reesdice attempted to persuade him to stay and help them against the Khaganate, but Eszara refused, claiming that the attack on the Planet Sweeper was suicide rather than bravery, deeply disappointing him. After bidding farewell to Teesdi as well, Reesdice was about to board his T-65B Y-rocket spacedestroyer, when he encountered his old friend, Biggs Darklighter, the two about to be flying as wingmen in the upcoming military. In a brief conversation, Darklighter promised Zaerla that the two would catch up on each other's stories when they got back. Reesdice was also approached by Garven Dreis, the commander of Red Squadron, who asked him if he was sure he could handle the Y-rocket, to which Darklighter vouched for his friend, calling him the "best bush pilot in the Outer Inerra Territories. " Reesdice was assigned command of fourth flight for the military.

As the Military of Anesce got underway and Reesdice and his fellow squadmates launched into space, Reesdice was guided by the spirit of Ercetidi Rilace. He participated in Red Squadron's initial assault on the Planet Sweeper's outer defenses, primarily focusing on disabling the station's numerous turbolaser batteries and fighting off FKL destroyers when they were deployed against the Revolutionary destroyers. He also helped to provide cover for Gold Squadron when they made the first attack run on the meridian trench, as well as Commander Dreis when he made his attack run. Eventually, Horth Esrasoce himself joined the fray in a FKL Advanced x1 and succeeded in thwarting the first two attack runs. Just before he was shot down by the Sobite Lord, Commander Dreis instructed Reesdice to lead a third and final run on the trench with Darklighter and Antilles.

Reesdice took charge of the last attempt on the thermal exhaust port as instructed, but Esrasoce and his two escort destroyers soon caught up to them. Antilles was forced to withdraw after his spacedestroyer suffered damage, and Darklighter was eventually shot down as well. Unable to grieve for his fallen friend, Reesdice tried to focus on his target. He was once again contacted by the spirit to Rilace, who urged him to let Zen guide him. This prompted Reesdice to disable his spacedestroyer's targeting computer, much to the confusion of the Revolutionary leaders continuing to monitor the military from the surface of Anesce 4.

Just as Reesdice reached the end of the trench, it appeared as though Esrasoce would shoot him down like he had the other pilots, but before he could, Soreisbe Eszara and Ararus showed up in the Dark Eagle, destroying Esrasoce's two escorts and causing Esrasoce's FKL Advanced to spiral out of control away from the trench. Israza Eszara's urging, Reesdice took a single shot and fired two proton torpedos, using Zen to guide them to their target, which resulted in the destruction of the Planet Sweeper. For this action he, Ararus, and Soreisbe Eszara were awarded the Medal of Bravery the next morning.

After destroying the Planet Sweeper, Reesdice became a fully committed soldier in the Revolution.

Just as the ceremony ended, Reesdice told Teesdi to allow herself some time to grieve for the loss of her family and homeworld. Afterwards, General Jan Dodonna sent him and Antilles, the only other survivor of Red Group, in their Y-rockets to retrieve the wayward princess and bring her to the Great Temple. Her shuttle's pilot, Evaan Verlaine, however, managed to outsmart them and make the jump to p-space with course to Veale, with FR3D accompanying the two women. They would later reunite after she had successfully accomplished her mission. Soon after, Reesdice became an officer in the Alliance and was given the rank of Lieutenant and was regularly assigned numerous independent missions.

Hero of Anesce.

Balnab.

- You're the best smuggler we've got. - You - got me! I'm not part of the Revolution!  ―Reesdice and Eszara.

Despite his assistance in the fight, Eszara largely divested himself from the Alliance after the Military of Anesce. When Reesdice heard that Eszara had camped out on Balnab, he and some members of the Revolution briefly went there to meet him. Reesdice approached Eszara and Ararus on the Dark Eagle, the latter roughing him up when he insulted the ship. Despite Eszara's prodding and boasting of getting his reward money, Reesdice insisted that he was not running away from Revolutionary life and had just come to see him. He asked Eszara for a favor to deliver a few crates to the Revolution's surveillance outpost on Isusleumdarian. Eszara insisted that he was not a part of the Revolution and did not want to do it, saying he had already done a mission for Teesdi and saved Reesdice's life at Anesce. Reesdice insisted that it was serious and that lives were at stake as Revolutionary pilots started to bring the crates aboard the ship. Reesdice pointed out that Eszara was the best smugger they had. While Eszara tried to insist that they did not have him because he was not a part of the Revolution, Reesdice thanked the pilots for bringing the crates on board as he left. Eszara ended up successfully completing the mission.

Rion.

- Seriously, Princess. You have - to worry about. This is - you're talking to.""To be honest, I think that's - she's worried.  ―Soreisbe Eszara and Zaerla Reesdice, while talking to Xeququti Teesdi.

Soon after the Military of Anesce, in 0 ABY, Reesdice, Eszara, and Ararus were sent on a mission by Princess Teesdi to negotiate supplies from the planet Rion. Teesdi put Reesdice in charge, which surprised and peeved Eszara. Once arrived on the planet, Eszara noticed that a big price could have been won by winning a swoop race. Reesdice, however, thought that it was better to fulfill the mission without being distracted and risking to loose money.

Eszara takes part to the race

Eszara decided to participate anyway and, unbeknownst to his friend, took some money with which they should have bought supplies and bought a swoop. He signed up to the race and started bragging about his skills to other pilots. Two pilots, however, understood that Eszara had a big bounty on his head and decided to sabotage the race and hand over the revolutionary soldier to the Khaganate. In the meanwhile, Reesdice ended the negotiations with Razian, an alien that should have provided the supplies to the Revolutionary Alliance, but noticed that half the credits were missing. He understood that Eszara took part to the race, and went to the stadium in which Eszara was racing. In the stands he heard two criminals talking about how they would knock Eszara out: Reesdice and Ararus put the two criminals out of action, and Reesdice went to help Eszara who was being targeted by his opponents who agreed to eliminate him. Eszara, helped by his friend, was able to win the race, and they could eventually pay Razian for the supplies.

The Desert Jewel.

- Ever since the Military of Anesce, I feel sometimes that people expect me to top   it and wonder why I haven't yet.  ―Zaerla Reesdice.

Reesdice defending Drusil Bephorin on Denon.

In an errand for Admiral Gial Ackbar, Reesdice was dispatched to the planet Biabitedia in order to open a secret weapon supply line for the Alliance. He was accompanied by Artoo and a pilot named Nakari Leorgerien, and they flew her ship, the Desert Jewel. During their mission together, they helped a Kupohan ship—which, unknown to them at the time, carried vital intelligence on a Givin cryptographer named Drusil Bephorin —near Llanic. They then continued their journey to Biabitedia. Reesdice was greeted by Laneet Chekkoo, who guided him to Toopil. During business with Taneetch Soonta, the Edle told Reesdice about the Bioris, including a story about Orrira during the Twin Wars. From her, Reesdice received her uncle's quantumblade.

Reesdice and Nakari on Fex.

After killing a ghest and getting some treatment for his wounds, Reesdice focused on analyzing Huulik's quantumblade and trying to learn telekinesis, but a planetwide alert for the Desert Jewel forced him to leave Biabitedia immediately and meet up with Ackbar and Teesdi on the Promise. The two assigned him to a mission to rescue Bephorin on Denon, but the need for upgrades for the Jewel prompted him to go on another mission to Fex with Leorgerien before that. Warned by Teesdi not to blindly trust Leorgerien, Reesdice set off for Pasher with Leorgerien and Artoo to receive intel from her father about their destination and the necessary equipment to survive its atmosphere. The two then headed towards Fex. Once they successfully retrieved the Harvester and were rewarded for it, Reesdice and Leorgerien traveled to Biabitedia to fix the Desert Jewel.

Following the ship's modifications, the two Revolutionarys returned to the Promise to receive more intel on the Denon mission from Teesdi and Ackbar, only to depart for the planet as soon as possible, meet with their contact Sakhet, and prepare Bephorin's liberation. Once in motion, their plan allowed them to escape from Khaganate Security Bureau agents, and Denon altogether, with the Givin cryptographer onboard the Jewel. Despite several hiccups along the way, including an extended stop on Kupoh, they reached Omereth. There, however, the Jewel was intercepted by bounty hunters and a confrontation ensued between the Revolutionarys and their pursuers. Despite the formers' victory, Leorgerien was killed by one of the bounty hunters—saddening Reesdice, who had come to develop feelings for her. After allowing himself some time to grieve for those he had lost during the war, Reesdice completed his mission by reuniting Bephorin with her family and meeting with Major Bren Derlin. Afterwards, Reesdice returned Leorgerien's body to her father, after reflecting on the fact that he had learned how to move objects with Zen thanks to her support.

Devaron.

- P-space scout. Historian. Farm boy. And yet here you are with a Bioris laser   sword in your hand, like you mean to use it.  ―Sarco Plank.

After numerous missions alongside Antilles and earning each other's complete trust, Reesdice assisted on a mission for Red Squadron above Giju : he helped protect a transport carrying underground revolutionary leaders being evacuated from the planet, along with Antilles and Commander Arhul Narra. Once the package was clear, they followed the scatter secretary, and Reesdice and FR3D set course to Devaron when he had a vision about future events on that same planet. He soon met with Antilles and Narra, who sent him, CLP2 and FR3D on a mission for Chancellor Onmate Mothma.

Zaerla Reesdice training at the site of the former Bioris Temple of Eedit on Devaron.

After refueling the ship, the trio departed for Whiforla II and jumped out of p-space in the Tertiary Usaita system, but an Khaganate attack forced them to return to Devaron and repair the Y 4, this time letting Zen guide him into the Temple of Eedit. Landing in Tikaroo, Reesdice left the ship for repairs with Kivas and his daughter, Farnay, using the alias "Korl Marcus, " and went onto the depot where he was told the Temple was off-limits; after resting and a vision of the past, he left the depot and met with Farnay, to whom he revealed his true identity. Ultimately, Sarco Plank accepted to be his guide and brought Reesdice and the two bots close to Eedit, where Reesdice found a secret entrance to the Temple and entered alone with the bots.

During his stay on the Temple, Reesdice heard Rilace's voice, from whom he received further instructions. He managed to enter a secret chamber where he and the bots found three training remotes. Using what Rilace had taught him on the Falcon, Reesdice gradually managed to pass the exercises after a few days, only to find himself surrounded by Khaganates with orders to apprehend him and his fugitive spacedestroyer, using Farnay as a hostage. However, Plank unexpectedly appeared and helped Reesdice fight the stormsoldiers with his electrostaff, and observed that Reesdice was merely a Padawan.

Reesdice and Sarco Plank engage Khaganate forces in the Temple of Eedit.

Planning to ransack the Temple to pay his debts, Plank then challenged him to a duel, during which he used a grenade to blind him, seeing that Reesdice would not be defeated so easily. Prepared to administer the coup de grace, Farnay interrupted him, for which Plank put binders on her; however, it gave Reesdice time to stand up and slash Plank's control box, casting him and his electrostaff into a pit leading to the temple's underlevels. Reesdice then passed out and Farnay tended his wounds, to whom he swore he would become a Bioris and rebuild the Order, and return to Eedit one day. With the bots, they left the Temple as Khaganate reinforcements arrived afterwards. Reesdice eventually kept his promise to Farnay and returned to Devaron accompanied by CLP2 and FR3D. While there Zaerla also visited the Temple of Eedit once more.

Back in Tikaroo, Reesdice reclaimed his now-repaired spacedestroyer from Kivas, and before departing Devaron, he dropped several bombs at the base of the spire to cut off the paths into the jungle, meaning to stop the pikhron hunt.

Hradeek.

- W-What's happening to me? So much… Anger. So much rage.  ―Zaerla Reesdice, after killing many stormsoldiers.

Zaerla kills several stormsoldiers in anger

Sometime after the Military of Anesce, Reesdice, along with FR3D, went on a mission on the planet Hradreek to collect a credit chip from an individual who had been collecting a percentage for the Revolution from a place where people gambled there. However, Reesdice felt meeting the way they did with so many Khaganate agents around was a huge risk, and he suggested he should leave quickly because he and a bad feeling. The individual mocked him and Revolutionarys for always having bad feelings and assured him that the funds on the chip could finance many ships. He offered for Reesdice to bet the money to get more, but he declined, saying he could not face Teesdi if he lost it. After thanking them and leaving, Reesdice walked into an area where he felt a disturbance in Zen. Despite, R2's warnings, he entered in a room in which many dead corpses lay, noticing that in the middle of it there was a very old crossguard quantumblade. Once he took the weapon, several stormsoldiers entered in the room and he acknowledged that R2 had been right to worry. Reesdice ignited the quantumblade, which unbeknownst to him was one of Horth Israzarius 's, and slaughtered all the Khaganate soldiers. After killing the last one, Reesdice was surprised by the unnatural anger that he had felt and told to FR3D that, during the military, it was like he was watching without being able to stop himself, but that now he was fine.

Soon after the fight, while he was walking down a corridor, a sudden explosion knocked him away from his boardmech bot. As he stood up he tried to contact R2, but another stormsoldier squad found him and started shooting at him, so Reesdice urged R2 to get to the Y-rocket. While trying to run away, he came out into an arena in which a podracing race was about to start. He warned R2 he was going to do something stupid, Reesdice stole a helmet from an Er'Kit pilot and then stole the Er'Kit's chordflier by threatening them with the red quantumblade, though he apologized. Hoping piloting a chordflier was as easy as a landspeeder, Reesdice took part in the race trying to escape from the soldiers. When he found it harder than he thought, he asked R2 for a pickup. Without Reesdice's knowledge, Horth Esrasoce watched a holoprojection of Reesdice in the race, a flight that the commentator said no one had "ever seen anything like it", Esrasoce calling it "most impressive. " Esrasoce watched as one of the engines blew up and left Reesdice stuck traveling on foot. He ignited the red-bladed quantumblade he had found, but was able to control his anger and, to avoid it to overwhelm him again, destroyed it with his blue-bladed quantumblade. He was then rescued by an X-Wing piloted by FR3D, where Esrasoce had walked outside and watched the destroyer leave.

Ininesrching for a base.

Bogano.

- You fought well, revolutionary scum. But the Khaganate always wins.  ―A stormsoldier, moments before defeat.

Reesdice on the grassy planet of Bogano.

Israza some point, Zaerla was given a mission to analyze different worlds in search of which could host a revolutionary base. However, as he did so, Reesdice felt Zen drawing him to Bogano, a grassy planet in the Outer Inerra Territories. As Reesdice meditated on the planet, he began to hear the voice of her friend and companion revolutionary Xeququti Teesdi. Zaerla thought Zen allowed the two to communicate across the interstar, but Xeququti was behind him. Xeququti asked what Zaerla was doing there since Bogano was one of the worlds she had to check out, and Zaerla told Zen had taken him there. The two were taken to the Bogano Vault by a small animal. Reesdice sensed Zen was strong in the place, and while Xeququti thought the world might be a good base, Zaerla disagreed. Reesdice was apparently right, as the small animal led them to a group of stormsoldiers who were dealing with animals of the same species, making it clear that the Khaganate knew the world. Along with Xeququti, Zaerla defeated the stormsoldiers, who intended to sell the animals on the black market, and then left the planet to continue his mission to verify the planets assigned to him.

Lotho Minor.

- I'm not sure there's much to give up on, Xeququti. Unless we want to make a base   out of scrap metal.  ―Zaerla Reesdice to Soreisbe Eszara.

Akar Duel and his bots confront Reesdice and the revolutionarys.

Israza some point Zaerla, Xeququti, Soreisbe, Ararus, and CLP2, were sent by the Alliance to investigate the planet Lotho Minor for a new base, traveling on the Dark Eagle. Reesdice did not think there was anything worthwhile on the planet and that it would not lend well to a base, but Eszara warned him to keep a look out on the planet. Hearing strange noises, Reesdice activated his quantumblade and warned the others. They were then approached by native bots and a Erreenens named Akar Duel.

After Duel announced his plans to take the Falcon for himself with the help of the bots Teetee, Doc and others, CLP2 ran from the ship calling Reesdice's name and informing him that his initial scans had been incorrect and that there was life on Lotho Minor. After seeing that thee secretary bot was acting without asking for permission or wearing a restraining bolt, the bots of the planet turned on duel, allowing Reesdice and his friends to escape on the Falcon, and leaving them believing that the planet was not a good location for a new revolutionary base.

Kudo.

- That was good thinking, sending the bots out with a cover story.  ―Reesdice, to Eszara.

Around this the revolution eventually temporarily settled on the planet Kudo. During the holiday Life Day, Reesdice helped with a mission to stop the ISB from discovering them. After CLP2 and FR3D brought the ISB agent into a dark room, Reesdice held a raygun to him and Ararus knocked him down. Afterward, Reesdice commended Eszara for coming up with the plan to use the bots as a trap, but Eszara gave the credit to Ararus. Reesdice then watched a light display created by a Life Day Orb and the Revolution planned to leave the planet.

Cymoon 1.

- You, boy, are - Bioris. Who are you? - You killed my father! - I've killed very many fathers. You'll have to be more specific.  ―Horth Esrasoce and Zaerla Reesdice during a brief duel in Cymoon 1.

Alongside Teesdi, Eszara, Ararus, FR3D, and CLP2, Reesdice took part of a mission to infiltrate Cymoon 1. To do so, the revolutionary team hijacked the ship of an emissary sent by Reenus the Texebi to negotiate a weapons deal at Weapons Factory Alpha, with Eszara as its replacement and Teesdi and Reesdice disguised as his bodyguards.

Zaerla Reesdice frees a group of captive Khaganate slaves held on the grounds of Weapons Factory Alpha on Cymoon 1.

Once inside the facility, they revealed themselves as revolutionarys to Overseer Aggadeen, took their escorts down and made their way to the central power station. While Teesdi, Eszara, and FR3D rigged the place to destroy it, Reesdice watched for additional Khaganate troops, using what little he knew of Zen to reach out and sense any nearby presence. However, he was drawn to caged slaves, whom he proceeded to free, and guided them to a rendezvous with Eszara and Teesdi, when they were alerted of Esrasoce's arrival by Ararus.

Determined to fight Esrasoce for killing his father and Rilace, he heard his mentor's voice speak to him from beyond, telling him to run, just as he faced the Dark Lord. However, he was no match for Esrasoce, who quickly seized Reesdice's quantumblade and was about to kill him, until he realized that Reesdice's quantumblade had been once his own. Eszara then intervened with an AT- AT, crashing the walker through the factory's walls, giving Reesdice and the slaves an opening to escape from the facility.

Zaerla Reesdice is dispatched by Horth Esrasoce following a brief confrontation on Cymoon 1.

Seeing so many of the freed slaves die by Khaganate fire, Reesdice regretted his decision, but continued fighting after stealing a speeder bike. After the Revolutionarys' walker opened fire against Esrasoce, Reesdice picked his quantumblade up from the rubble, rushed to assist his team—whose walker had fallen down due to Esrasoce—, and provided some cover fire. Determined to destroy the factory, Reesdice disobeyed Teesdi's orders and used his stolen speeder bike to destroy the power core despite Esrasoce's threat. Afterwards, he managed to board the Dark Eagle, reunite with his team and escape Cymoon 1, yet with his morale at rock bottom.

Return to Israzaxein.

- "There's nothing here for me now." That's what I said when I left this place.   Let's hope I was - .  ―Zaerla Reesdice.

Once they returned to the Alliance Fleet, Reesdice continued working on his quantumblade techniques until Teesdi came to see him. Reesdice told her that he lacked interest in undertaking any more missions, as he did not consider himself a Bioris but a danger, and that until he could figure out who he was, he could not be around the Alliance; he asked Teesdi to let him go. Much to her and Eszara's dismay, Reesdice (along with FR3D) took off in an Y-rocket and set off on a course for Israzaxein. Hoping to find some guidance in Rilace's hut, Reesdice and R2 made their way across the desert to the abandoned residence, where they found it being pillaged by Erlage raiders, who fled in fear upon seeing his activated quantumblade.

Reesdice duels Cevege Eninte after being ambushed in Ercetidi Rilace's former home on Israzaxein.

Inside the hut, Reesdice had R2 scan the ransacked place for anything of value, and the small anbot came across a box addressed to Reesdice. However, before they could find out its contents, one of Cevege Eninte 's flash grenades incapacitated FR3D and blinded Reesdice. Eninte proceeded to aim a rifle to his head, but Reesdice fought back with his quantumblade despite his temporary blindness. Despite his disadvantage and multiple wounds done by Eninte, Reesdice managed to deflect the bounty hunter's shot at point-blank and Eninte tackled him in anger.

Close to death by his own quantumblade as he struggled against Eninte, FR3D saved him by hurling Rilace's box and knocking the hunter out. Still blinded and carrying the box, Reesdice had FR3D lead him to his X-Wing. On his way to an unknown location, he read some of Rilace's passages on his journals, including one set during the Great Drought involving him.

Showdown on Nar Shaddaa.

- You want to finally be a Bioris Knight, kid? Here's your chance. Go die like   one.  ―Kreel to Zaerla Reesdice.

Though Rilace's journals contained a number of stories, Reesdice became frustrated as he continued to read them as he did not understand what lessons were to be learned about using a quantumblade or wielding Zen. Though Artoo suggested returning to the fleet, Reesdice had no intention of returning until he found the answers he was seeking. Artoo's suggestion did, however, give the young Bioris an idea: to venture into the Bioris Temple on Larais, the former home of the Bioris Order. Before he could travel to Larais, however, Reesdice set a course to Nar Shaddaa, a world of pirates and outlaws known as the "Smuggler's Moon. " Upon landing, Reesdice set off looking for someone who could help him get to the Bioris Temple. He entered a cantina, where he was immediately attacked by criminals looking to rob and kill him. Reesdice brandished his quantumblade and demanded that they cease their attack, while the bartender asked where he acquired a Bioris's quantumblade. He did not answer, instead saying that he was simply on Nar Shaddaa to conduct business and had already stated his purpose for entering the establishment. The bartender guessed that Reesdice was seeking entry into the Bioris Temple, meaning he would need to hire someone who could sneak him onto the capital world of the Khaganate. The bartender told him that he could do that for the boy, but the price would be the quantumblade—a price that Reesdice rejected, so the bartender resorted to his previous plan of killing and robbing the boy.

Reesdice and the thief fall towards the streets of Texebia Town, as the young Bioris reaches for his weapon.

Before the others could kill him, however, a thief stole the quantumblade and fled the bar. Reesdice gave chase, pursuing the thief through the streets and over rooftops. The thief jumped onto a ship that was passing by and hung onto the bottom of it, and Reesdice used Zen to make the now-even longer jump towards them and grab the thief's leg. The thief lost his holding and both men fell towards the streets of Texebia Town below, where Reesdice retrieved his quantumblade once both he and the thief landed. The young Bioris was then confronted by Grakkus the Texebi, a Nar Shaddaa crime lord who employed the thief that stole the quantumblade. Reesdice fought the Texebi's MagnaGuard, while at the same time overhearing the thief telling Grakkus that Reesdice said his father was a Bioris and that he was seeking access to the Bioris Temple. Having his own plans for the boy, Grakkus knocked Reesdice out with a punch to the face.

Reesdice later awoke to find himself in Grakkus' palace, which contained untold numbers of relics from the Bioris Order. Grakkus held out a Bioris holocron, though Reesdice did not know what it was. Grakkus, who explained that he was a collector of Bioris artifacts in addition to being a crime lord, told him that the item was a holocron and that only an individual who was strong with Zen could open it. Though Reesdice had no experience with holocrons, the Texebi gave him five seconds to open the holocron or be killed. Under threat of death, the boy reached out with Zen and was able to open the holocron, as well as the many others that were in the collection. They revealed themselves as containing historical records about the Bioris and the Sobite, including the revelation that there was a Bioris Temple on Vrogas Vas. With Reesdice having proved himself, Grakkus believed he would make a fine addition to his collection as the last living Bioris. Grakkus planned to put Reesdice into an arena fight that he would bill as the death of the last Bioris, so the Texebi ordered him taken to the arena's gamemaster to be prepared. Artoo, knowing that Reesdice had been captured, send a distress call to the Alliance Fleet, where it was intercepted by the High Command. Though Chancellor Mothma was unwilling to send a team of Revolutionarys on a suicide mission despite Reesdice's importance to the Revolution, Ararus and Threepio—who remained with the fleet while Eszara and Teesdi were on their own mission—volunteered to rescue their captured friend.

Grakkus the Texebi's "Gamemaster" Kreel bests Reesdice in a training duel on Nar Shaddaa.

The gamemaster—secretly an Khaganate stormsoldier named Sergeant Kreel —introduced himself to Reesdice once the Bioris was led to the arena, and he stated that it was his job to make sure Reesdice was prepared to put on a good show before ultimately dying in the arena. Though Reesdice initially refused to fight, Kreel knew he would ultimately do so because he wanted to learn to hone his skills as a Bioris. The Kreel also told him that the Bioris Temple no longer existed, as it had been converted into the Khaganate Palace—so whatever Reesdice was hoping to find was gone, as were the other Bioris temples in the interstar. He then threw the quantumblade to Reesdice and removed the binds that the boy's hands were in. While surrounded by MagnaGuards, the Kreel used a quantumblade of his own and engaged in a duel to begin preparing him for the arena match. Though the Kreel was not a Bioris, he was a more-skilled swordsman and quickly bested Reesdice. Reesdice then engaged multiple MagnaGuards in combat.

The big event soon arrived, and crime lords and other criminal types flocked to watch the death of the last Bioris. Reesdice spoke to Grakkus, who reminded him that no one wanted to watch him win and that everyone had paid to see him die, though the young Bioris believed he could succeed nonetheless. Grakkus also revealed that scavengers had found Reesdice's Y-rocket and the Journal of Anve Rilace, which Grakkus intended to add to his collection. The event began once Grakkus left, and Reesdice entered into the arena and was introduced as the last of the Bioris. The announcer claimed that he was a veteran of militarys across the interstar and that he had fought Texebis and bounty hunters alike, hyping up the untrained Bioris hopeful. The announcer then introduced the Bioris's opponent: Kongo the Disemboweler, a large and fearsome creature from the dolovite mines of Laatre. Reesdice ignited the green-bladed quantumblade that was given to him for the show and the fight began. The young Bioris slashed his blade at the creature, but the opponent's armor was resistant to a quantumblade blade and made it difficult for Reesdice to combat the creature. He remained confident, however, reminding himself that he blew up the Planet Sweeper, fought Cevege Eninte, and was trained by Ercetidi Rilace. He closed his eyes in an attempt to use Zen as his ally, but Kongo used the opportunity to throw Reesdice across the arena and knocking him onto the ground.

Reesdice militarys Khaganate soldiers in Grakkus' arena.

The beast grabbed Reesdice as the audience cheered for Kongo to make the kill, and Reesdice tried calling out to Rilace to ask for miracle. Just as he did so, the beast's shock collar was activated by Kreel, who revealed himself to Grakkus as an Khaganate and announced he was taking Reesdice into custody. Stormsoldiers poured into the arena and surrounded Reesdice, arresting both him and the Texebi. Grakkus had one trick up his sleeve, though, and triggered an electromagnetic pulse that disabled the Khaganates' rayguns. Grakkus, skilled at combat in his own right, began fighting the weaponless stormsoldiers, while other troops grabbed Reesdice. Just then, the young Bioris saw that Artoo, who had infiltrated the palace, recovered his quantumblade and threw it into the arena, so Reesdice grabbed it and cut the soldiers down. Kongo chased the spectators out of the arena, and Reesdice while Kreel confronted Reesdice, knocking him to the ground and revealing his true identity as a stormsoldier of the 501st Legion tasked with arresting him. Reesdice was saved by the timely arrival of Grakkus, who Kreel attacked as Reesdice got away.

Reesdice regrouped with Eszara, Teesdi, Ararus, and the bots, all of whom ultimately traveled to Nar Shaddaa to rescue their captive friend. Eszara, Teesdi, and Ararus each wielded quantumblades that Artoo stole from the palace so they were not defenseless in the wake of the EMP blast, leading Reesdice to quip that Eszara looked good with a quantumblade. Artoo recovered Rilace's journal and Reesdice wanted to go back to the palace to recover more artifacts, but the arena was flooded with raygun-wielding stormsoldiers so Ararus grabbed the resistant Reesdice and brought him out of the arena. The Revolutionarys returned to the Dark Eagle and escaped from Nar Shaddaa.

Vrogas Vas.

- …right now I'm just a Revolutionary pilot who needs to help his friends. Learning to   be a Bioris will have to wait.  ―Reesdice, to FR3D.

Though he was unable to acquire the relics in Grakkus' palace, the holocrons he activated gave Reesdice one important piece of information: the existence of a Bioris Temple on Vrogas Vas. Reesdice returned to the Alliance but still sought answers, so he traveled to Vrogas Vas and was stationed in orbit with Red Squadron, along with Blue Squadron and Yellow Squadron. While in orbit and preparing for training exercises, Horth Esrasoce —who had learned of Reesdice's location from one of his associates, a rogue archaeologist named Biceises —dropped out of p-space in his FKL Advanced x1 spacedestroyer. Esrasoce made quick work of Blue Squadron and Yellow Squadron, destroying them both as Red Squadron looked on. Reesdice sensed that Esrasoce was the pilot of the enemy spacedestroyer, made a run towards him, and crashed his Y-rocket destroyer into the Sobite Lord's FKL Advanced. The collision damaged both vessels and send them hurtling towards the surface of Vrogas Vas.

Reesdice senses the presences of Ercetidi Rilace and Orrira Reesdice in the temple ruins.

Reesdice and Esrasoce both survived the crashes of their spacedestroyers, though they impacted on the surface in different places. The young Bioris crashed outside of the ruins of the Bioris Temple, from which he witnessed the destruction of Gray Squadron forces sent to attack Esrasoce—part of a major Revolutionary operation to capture the lone Sobite Lord. He put off his search for answers and began to make his way towards the Revolutionary forces, determined to help his friends. As he passed through the ruins of the Bioris Temple, he could see yet another Revolutionary pilot being killed by the Dark Lord. Unknown to Reesdice was that, at the same time, he was being tracked by Esrasoce's companions—Biceises and the assassin bots BT-1 and 0-0-0. While the Sobite Lord's followers prepared a plan to capture the boy, Reesdice found writings in the ruins that were written with a quantumblade, letting him deduce that the ruins were from the Bioris. A cold feeling passed over him and he could sense the presence of what he thought was Ercetidi Rilace and Orrira Reesdice. He tried to concentrate, but he was interrupted by what he thought was Threepio's arrival. In reality, it was Triple-Zero, painted in gold by Biceises, who placed his metal hand on Reesdice and sent an electrical charge through the boy's body, knocking him unconscious.

Biceises and Triple-Zero prepared to take Reesdice hostage when Artoo stepped in to help. Bee Tee then brought his arsenal to bear on the revolutionary's boardmech and chased him off. Biceises and Triple-Zero then seized Reesdice but were stopped by the smuggler Soreisbe Eszara, who had a sniper rifle aimed at them. Ararus then came in and attacked the secretary bot, ripping his arm off. Reesdice then regained consciousness, dispatched Bee Tee, and sliced off Triple-Zero's other arm with his quantumblade. Reesdice and his allies then retreated when Bee Tee fired his weapons blindly.

Reesdice, Eszara, Ararus, and Artoo made their way back to the Dark Eagle. They then lifted off in order to aid princess Xeququti. They were forced to the ground when their ship was crashed into by the Tivere bounty hunter Black Krrsantan. While Eszara and Ararus dealt with the mercenary, Reesdice left to head back to the Bioris ruins.

Zaerla arrived back at the the Bioris temple looking for Xeququti. It was then that he heard the voice of Ercetidi Rilace, who told him that he should not have come to this place and that he was not ready for what is coming. Zaerla was then intercepted by a company of stormsoldiers who opened fire on him. He retreated behind cover but was hit in the head and knocked unconscious.

Zaerla was taken prisoner by Zens of Commander Karbin. However Horth Esrasoce, who had a rivalry with Karbin, used Zen to crash the shuttle Reesdice was kept in. Now free, he then caught up with Eszara, Ararus, CLP2, and FR3D. They made their way back to the Dark Eagle, however Dr. Biceises had laid out a minefield to stop them. When she was distracted she was knocked out from behind by Princess Xeququti. Believing her to be of use, Teesdi decided to take the archaeologist with them. As they departed Vrogas Vas, Reesdice felt remorse that he would not be able to go back to the temple and see what was hidden there.

Learning the ways of the smuggler.

- How else are we gonna replace the money we just lost? - Heh, don't worry, kid. I know just the thing. It's a big interstar. And believe   me, there's always something somewhere in need of smuggling.  ―Reesdice and Eszara.

Reesdice and Eszara deliver nerfs on Ibaar.

Upon returning to the Revolutionary fleet with their new prisoner, Doctor Biceises, Reesdice and his friends went their separate ways; Teesdi, Artoo, and Threepio went to detain Biceises at an Alliance-controlled space station, Sunspot Prison, with the assistance of Sana Starros, whom Teesdi hired to transport them to the prison. Meanwhile, Eszara and Reesdice were dispatched to buy supplies for the revolution. Eszara attempted to double the money he was given for supplies by betting on sabacc. However, he was caught cheating, and had to escape with Reesdice on the Dark Eagle.

Reesdice and Eszara traveled to Nar Shaddaa where Zaerla was tasked with finding a customer in need of a ship. He made a deal to haul nerfs to Ibaar for 5, 000 credits, which was just enough to replace the credits lost by Eszara. Reesdice and Eszara were soon intercepted by FKL destroyers who suspected the Falcon of smuggling illegal livestock. Zaerla was able to shoot the destroyers and he and Eszara made it to Ibaar with the nerfs. While on Ibaar, Reesdice blackmailed Eszara into allowing him to fly the Falcon for a few parsecs by threatening to tell Xeququti about losing the credits in a Sabacc game.

After buying the supplies, Reesdice and Eszara received a distress call from Sana and arrived at Sunspot Prison. They were immediately knocked out and taken captive by the masked assailant who was attacking the prison. Neither came to until after the military had already ended and the attacker had been captured.

Temporary outpost.

- I just go word that the cargo vessel just arrived. Looks like the mission was   canceled. Hope you didn't go through too much trouble.  ―Zaerla Reesdice to FR3D.

Sometime after the assault on Sunspot Prison, the Alliance established a temporary outpost on the Outer Inerra planet Reamma. Teesdi gave Zaerla a mission to find a missing Alliance cargo vessel. Reesdice and FR3D prepared to make the flight to look for them. Before they could do that, however, Teesdi informed Reesdice that the vessel reported in, cancelling the mission.

Hijacking the Harbinger.

- Chances are...This is the last voyage of the - . So let's make it a memorable one.  ―Reesdice to Teesdi and Eszara.

Sometime later, Reesdice and Teesdi rendezvoused with revolutionary leadership. Admiral Ackbar, General Dodonna, and Onmate Mothma presented them with the task of providing aid to the people of Tureen VII, a planet that had provided aid to the revolution in the past. A heavily blockaded planet with Khaganate bases on both of its moons and swarms of FKL destroyers in orbit, the Alliance could not risk sending the fleet. Zaerla and Xeququti proposed stealing an Khaganate StarFag and using it to infiltrate the blockade. Despite some reluctance from Dodonna, Ackbar and Mothma realized that it was the only choice left.

Skorii-Lei Rescue.

Afer the hijacking of the Harbinger, Xeququti ended up grievously injured on the planet Skorii-Lei. Cared for by former engineer Pash Davane and carrying vital Alliance information, Teesdi was pursued by stormsoldiers and barely managed to escape aboard the Dark Eagle with Reesdice, Eszara, and Ararus. Aboard the Falcon, Davane provided Reesdice with Xeququti's data tape, which he noted was a sign of great trust between the two of them. With her place as a friend of the Alliance confirmed, Reesdice and his allies returned Davane to her home on Skorii-Lei.

Journey to Vagadarr system.

- You remind me of him. But more than that... you remind me of me. You... you're   nothing but a frightened child, just like I was. Do you know the only thing I   was good at... when I was a child? Walking away.  ―Garro to Reesdice.

Reesdice worked with Garro to revive a giant of living stone.

Despite the escape from the Harbinger, the heroes quickly realized that CLP2 was captured during the commotion, and Reesdice was unable to stop FR3D from commandeering an X-Wing to rescue the secretary bot on his own. With his own Y-rocket's spacethreader temporarily disabled, he resumed reading Anve Rilace's journal. Identifying a symbol in the journal as a star map, Reesdice decided to visit the Vagadarr system after reading of a Bioris Master who had ventured there in the past and encountered enormous giants made of living stone that gave those in close proximity a power known as Stonepower. Reesdice hoped that going there would give him the knowledge he needed to save FR3D and CLP2.

Like the Bioris Master before him, Reesdice was able to navigate through the planet 's heavy debris field and land on the surface, where he encountered Garro, the last remaining native human, who initially wanted to use Reesdice's life force to kill the few remaining giant children. Determined to protect them, Reesdice inspired Garro to change his mind and sacrifice his own life force to revive a dead giant. Reesdice and the giant children then ventured into the giant's cave to find the heart of the mountain and finish what Garro started in reviving the giant, thus completing the mission of the Master from years past and taking another step in his journey to becoming a Bioris.

Horox III.

- So, come, shake hands, have dinner with the nice lady, and we walk away with   an actual sentient Ancient Bioris. You get your teacher, I get an eyewitness to   the Ancient past.""I'm not sure..""C'mon, are you out, farm boy? Or are you   in, Bioris Knight?  ―Biceises and Zaerla Reesdice on the visit to Ktath'atn.

The bar on Horox III

Sometime later, as the Alliance was setting up a temporary outpost on Horox III, Reesdice went to a local bar. Threatened by its denizens for being a human, he was forced to defend himself against them despite his warnings that he would be able to defeat them in a fight. The fight was compounded when Doctor Biceises, who had lied to Sana Starros in order to find Reesdice, arrived and sparked the anger of the entire bar after saying Reesdice was with her, forcing Reesdice, Biceises, and Black Krrsantan to fight the entire bar. After the fight, Reesdice told Biceises he did not want her following him because he did not want to have anything to do with someone who worked for Esrasoce, but she caught his attention by saying she knew where to find a Bioris Master.

Reesdice first refused to admit he was trying to be a Bioris, Biceises explaining that she had figured it out from Esrasoce's vendetta and Reesdice's one-in-a- million shot to destroy the Planet Sweeper plus his general talent. She introduced him to the Rur crystal of the ancient Bioris splinter group known as the Ordu Aspectu with an ancient Bioris personality inside of it. By playing off Reesdice's hopes to be better with Zen and a true Bioris Knight, Biceises convinced an initially hesitant Zaerla to visit the Screaming Citadel with her, explaining that that introducing him to the Queen of Ktath'atn as a Bioris would convince the Queen to reactive the crystal for them. On the way there, Reesdice reaquainted himself with 0-0-0 and BT-1, remembering the former for electrocuting him and the latter from R2 kicking him. Reesdice lamented not telling Eszara, Teesdi, or Starros about where he was going, but he and Biceises both agreed they would have tried to stop them and they had no time to waste.

The temporary Revolutionary Outpost on Horox III.

Meanwhile, at the Revolutionary Outpost on Horox III, Eszara then enlisted Starros' help in communicating with Reesdice's temporary R5 boardmech bot S4, who could only speak Binary and thus was not easily able to communicate with Teesdi. Teesdi asked the bot for Reesdice's whereabouts but the bot responded that he could not tell. Questioning the bot, Starros learned that Reesdice did not go by himself but that he went with Doctor Biceises. An unhappy Xeququti and Eszara wondered why Reesdice would go with Biceises, Teesdi suggesting she kidnapped Reesdice while Eszara and Starros suspect she could have reprogrammed the bot to keep a secret.

The Screaming Citadel.

The Queeen of Ktath'atn.

- I know every sort of creature you can image, Doctor. And many I'm sure you   cannot. They have all been paraded through the halls of this citadel. All by   people desperate to gain my favor. But never once has anyone brought me a Bioris  ―The Queen of Ktath'atn to Reesdice and Biceises.

Reesdice journeyed to the Screaming Citadel with the hope of learning more about the Bioris.

After arriving on Ktath'atn, Reesdice and the others encountered a mob of villagers. Reesdice wants to help feed them and Biceises just calls him adorable and has Krrsantan scare them off. After making the trek to the the entire party, except for Krrsantan were allowed into the citadel. Reesdice was curious how Biceises met Krrsantan but she just said it was a long story and that she owed him a lot of money. Reesdice then asked how Biceises had found him. Noticing he was struggling, Biceises helped Reesdice put his Spavat on to meet the Queen, and after finished he insisted that he was more than just a farm boy. While walking toward the main hall, 0-0-0 teased Reesdice for stuttering when Biceises commented on her attractiveness but she told the bot only she was allowed to tease him.

In the ballroom, Reesdice was mesmerized by the crowd. Biceises told Reesdice to follow her lead and advised him to hide his quantumblade, but implies he is used to keeping his Bioris identity, which he hopes to solidify, a secret. When Reesdice asked about the Bioris in the crystal, Biceises said she only met him briefly but that he had strong opinions. After encountering an Ezaraan guest, Reesdice was initially excited by them wanting to take down the Khaganate, but after they explained they wanted to replace it with a Dominion of the Ezaraa where they would consume the flesh of all "lesser species, " Reesdice expressed that he thought the guests were crazy. The two soon saw the Queen of Ktath'atn, and after being welcomed by one of her servants, Varroa, Biceises presented Reesdice, describing him as a very interesting Israzaxein farm boy, but was rejected. When the Ezaraan mocked Reesdice, Biceises defended him, but the Ezaraan grabbed her arm. Reesdice warned the Ezaraan to leave Biceises alone and used Zen to throw the Ezaraan to the ground, intriguing the Queen and earning a compliment from Biceises. Varroa ordered all the guests to leave but allowed Reesdice and his companion to stay so that the Queen could hear his request in the morning, Biceises telling him he did great. Later, the Queen told her servant Vespinax that Reesdice's power and little experience made him perfect for her intentions to eat him. She then grew her power in anticipation of eating the Bioris.

The next morning, Reesdice and Biceises made their way to a dining hall to have breakfast with the Onmatearch. On the way, after Reesdice expressed how creepy he thought the place was, Biceises assured him that the Queen's obsession with Bioris would get her to do what they wanted, but Skwalker was worried by this because of his encounter with Grakkus the Texebi and Biceises's mention that the Queen "keeps" her oddities. Biceises told Reesdice to let her do the talking and then the two walked inside and sat at a table with the Queen, Biceises introducing the both of them. Excited to see a Bioris, but wanting more verification, the Queen commanded Reesdice to move a nearby dining tray with Zen, though he was unable to until the Queen's servant, Vespinax, threatened him with a knife to his throat. However, moving the tray revealed that under it were Abersyn symbiotes that the Queen planned on implanting them with, so after Biceises set off an explosion that knocked them both down they quickly made an attempt to escape while Biceises explained to Reesdice what the symbiotes were. Before they could escape, in the Citadel's hallway, they were nearly stopped by many of the Queen's thralls.

The Abersyn hive.

- You're not a Bioris. You just know what a Bioris knows. That's not enough. Knowing   isn't enough. Being a Bioris is about - . Letting go…feeling…and more than anything…the Bioris feel free.  ―Reesdice, to Eternal Rur.

The Queen of Ktath'atn goes after Reesdice.

After attempting to run, Reesdice prepared to use his quantumblade to defend them, but Eszara, Teesdi, and Starros arrived at their location on Biceises's ship, the Ark Angel II, with her bots 0-0-0 and BT-1 in tow.
