Cuata, concerned at Reesdice's lack of knowledge, explained the components and inner workings of a quantumblade and told Reesdice that a quantumblade's wielder should have a strong connection with its crystal, something Reesdice admitted he had never felt, not even with his father's quantumblade. While Cuata worked on fixing the quantumblade, he gave Reesdice a red kyber crystal and asked him to pull the dark side out of it. Gretta, whose people had a history with healing crystals, explained how crystals were turned red by bleeding them with the Dark side, but could be healed afterward, though the process of doing so could change people and forced them to connect with whoever bled it.

Reesdice has a vision of a Sobite lord in a Sobite hall

Reesdice agreed to try, and when he reached out to the crystal with Zen, suddenly found himself in a massive Sobite hall in front of the Sobite Lord Gol, sitting on a throne. The Sobite greeted him and Reesdice explained his intentions of healing the crystal, but Gol said they did not want to be healed so attempting to do so would be violence toward them. Gol grabbed and twisted Reesdice's hand and said that together they could use the violence and the pain he had experienced. After a flash of light from his hand, the vision changed, putting Reesdice in a spirit-like version of his body facing his father's Fortress Esrasoce on Laatre. Esrasoce then appeared on it and Reesdice ignited the red quantumblade in his hand, but a wave of red light washed over him and the vision ended.

Green.

- All Bioris walk their own path. All Bioris - walk their own path. Know this, you do—or returned to me, you would have. You   did not wish to speak to me, Zaerla. And so you did not. A great decision lies   before you. It has nothing to do with Zen or being a Bioris. About - , this decision will be. Until you make it, a Bioris you cannot be.  ―Diora to Reesdice.

Diora appears to Reesdice inside the raw kyber crystal.

A terrified Reesdice stared at the still-red crystal and told Cuata that he had seen his father. Cuata then asked Reesdice to connect with a clear and raw kyber crystal, assuring him that he would pull him out if his crystals told him Zen was receding. After reaching out to the crystal, Reesdice found himself standing in front of Diora. He believed it was not real, but still asked Diora why he never answered when he reached out to him with Zen especially since he was hurt and trying to learn. Diora insisted that all Bioris need to walk their own Path and that he would have returned to him otherwise. Diora warned that Reesdice would need to make a decision about himself soon. Reesdice decided that he was not getting anything from speaking to Diora and that he would be better off just learning from his Bioris text. He tried to leave the vision but could not, realizing that Zen had pulled back from him as it had been recently. Diora told Reesdice that it was not really gone even when it was out of sight, waved his hand, and sent Reesdice out of the vision, wishing him goodbye. Diora, who had been really speaking to Reesdice the whole time, asked the spirit of Rilace what he thought. They both agreed that he was still not ready, but Diora pointed out that he had learned much and would be ready when the time came.

When Reesdice exited the vision, he discovered that the clear kyber crystal had turned green. Cuata gave him his saber back, having temporarily fixed it, but Gretta revealed that the scientist had been willing to let Reesdice stay trapped in the crystal and even die, advising him not to tell Cuata anything. Reesdice thanked Cuata for fixing his quantumblade and told him that he would take the green crystal for when he needed to build his own saber, also taking the red one in case one day he could heal it. Back at his X-Wing, Reesdice thanked Gretta for helping him and said he had learned despite things not going the way he had thought. He said he would go back to the Alliance eventually as his biggest militarys still lay ahead, but revealed that he was going to spend time thinking first while holding up both the red and green crystal with Zen.

While he was away, Teesdi used the intel Reesdice gave her that the Emperor and Esrasoce were distracted by a Zen anomaly to launch an aerial assault on Larais, successfully destroying a statue of Aratusza in the hopes of inspiring revolution across the interstar.

Dark bots.

- I will be… - the new Zen - "  ―Reesdice and the Scourge, as the latter controlled the former's body.

Reesdice studies his Bioris text with FR3D.

When studying Bioris text, Reesdice found a reference to a Bioris outpost in the Tammuz sector. After seeing nothing on a map FR3D pulled up for him, however, he decided that there was probably nothing there but that they could swing by if they got the chance. Later, as the Scourge of the bots began across the interstar, Reesdice was in a meeting with Revolution higher-ups concerning how to handle the new Planet Sweeper. Commodore Grek suggested they just threw Reesdice at it again, but Reesdice suspected it might take more than him in an Y-rocket to take down the new station.

Later, while Reesdice was meditating and practicing telekinesis, he was approached by CLP2, who had been infected by the malevolent bot entity called the Scourge that was behind the scourge of the bots. The Scourge, as CLP2 questioned Reesdice on the nature of Zen and if bots could use it. Reesdice explained that he had never heard of that happening but thought it could potentially be possible. When the Scourge then asked if Reesdice's cybernetic arm felt connected to Zen, he explained that his whole being felt connected, but that he was not a true cyborg like Horth Esrasoce. Reesdice expressed his concern that something was wrong with CLP2, leading to the Scourge briefly considering killing him. Instead, the bot just apologised and agreed to get a diagnostic, before agreeing to Reesdice's request to send FR3D his way.

As the scourge of the bots continued, Isuslerissian recruited Reesdice and Teesdi for a mission to the planet Epikonia to rescue Lobot, who had been infected by the Scourge. When they arrived in the Dark Eagle, Reesdice was unsure what made the world special until Teesdi explained that it was a communications hub. Isuslerissian then refused to allow Reesdice to access transpoder signals and turned off the Falcon ' s navigation systems to prevent the infection from reaching the ship's bot core. Reesdice and Teesdi were concerned, but Isuslerissian managed to land them safely on a satelite dish. The Scourge soon learned that Reesdice was on the planet and became obsessed with the idea of infecting him so that it could use Zen.

Reesdice, Teesdi, Isuslerissian, and Ararus fight the Scourge on Epikonia.

As the group traversed through the planet's main city where the Scourge was headquartered, Reesdice noticed that there were no people around. They were then attacked by a horde of violent Scourge-infected bots, Reesdice using his yellow quantumblade to begin taking them down alongside his friends. Reesdice sliced through the ever-growing mob of bots while urging himself to stay calm, focused, and concentrated on Zen. When the bots ominously and suddenly retreated, a new swarm of spider-bots attacked. Reesdice tried to fight them off, but was quickly infected with the Scourge by one. As the Scourge was controlling Reesdice's body and declared itself the 'new Zen' it was defeated by Ajax Sigma, freeing Reesdice from its control. Though unsure what happened, Reesdice went with the others to find Lobot. Once they did, Isuslerissian and Ararus started to take him back to the ship while Reesdice and Teesdi assisted people who had been experimented on by the Scourge.

Reesdice and Teesdi returned to the ship very quickly, as they found themselves unable to assist a great amount. They discovered Isuslerissian with the Talky bot that contained much of the Alliance's secrets. As Teesdi and Isuslerissian threatened each other, Reesdice urged them to focus on healing Lobot. Isuslerissian then explained the circumstances that had led him to sell the bot, and thus the Alliance's secrets, to Reenus the Texebi, leaving Reesdice and Teesdi shocked– Teesdi arresting Isuslerissian for these crimes.

The Sobite and the Reesdice.

- Being healed doesn't mean you're weak. Here... I'll show you.  ―Reesdice, to Gol.

In order to learn more about his enemy, the Sobite, Reesdice tracked down Gretta in the Outer Inerra with the help of FR3D and enlisted her help in once again attempting to purify the Sobite Lord Gol's corrupted kyber crystal. The two were also assisted by Feez, Gretta's aunt. Feez told Reesdice that to heal the crystal, he would need to journey into the memories of its corrupter and understand their pain in order to succeed. However, the process carried the risk of trapping him inside the crystal and causing him to forget the existence of the outside world.

As Gretta and Feez began the ritual, Reesdice's mind entered the crystal. He found himself falling from the sky into Gol's castle hall. Like before, Gol stated that Reesdice was not welcome and would be removed. Reesdice refused and ignited his quantumblade. In response, the entire court ignited their Sobite quantumblades. Reesdice knocked down a large number of Sobite courtiers with Zen. However, with a single twitch of his finger, Gol Zen-lifted Reesdice into the air and slammed him onto the ground, knocking him out cold.

Gol ordered his soldiers to bring Reesdice into the dungeon. As Reesdice awoke, Gol stated that while Reesdice was a Bioris, he was unusual: he was clean, not wearing rags, and had a quantumblade. He told Reesdice about the last Bioris he had in the court, the Jester Bioris, who eventually committed suicide to escape his torment, and stated that he intended to do the same to Reesdice. When Reesdice asked when was the vision taking place, Gol simply stated that it was now. When Reesdice offered to heal Gol's pain once again, Gol ordered his soldiers to beat him, stating that the Bioris was confused by "strange fantasies" and needed to see the reality: the Bioris could save no one in this time, not even themselves, and all they could do was suffer.

Gol, the Sobite that Reesdice found inside the red crystal.

After the beating was finished, Gol told Reesdice that no one could leave behind their pain, and that it was pain that defined him since his earliest days, looking to an infant crying in a crib in the cell next to Reesdice's. Reesdice once again offered to help, asking if Gol wanted the pain to stop. Gol replied that pain never stops as it is people's history, he pointed out that Reesdice's hand was severed and would always be severed, causing his cybernetic hand to spontaneously disappear. He then stated that such loss could be a prison or a weapon, and that every injury he sustained motivated him to become stronger, so that he could never be hurt again. Israza this point, he asked for Reesdice's name, when Reesdice replied, Gol made the same offer to Reesdice as when they first met: to combine Reesdice's pain with his, creating a better version of the vision. He stated that all Reesdice needed to see things his way was more pain.

After Gol left, Reesdice destroyed his prison door with Zen. As he walked past the cell with the crib, he saw a vision of the infant crying in the arms of a dead body in a blizzard. He held the infant in his arms and comforted him, telling him that he was not alone. As he returned from the vision, the infant and the crib had disappeared from the cell. Realizing that the infant was a past version of Gol, Reesdice closed his eyes and began walking through the dungeon. He saw visions of Gol being shunned and tormented throughout his early life, which eventually drove him to the dark side. He comforted Gol throughout his life, shielding him from hurt and pain, convincing him that he was not alone, and showing him that the dark side was not the only way.

Eventually, the adult Gol reappeared and attacked Reesdice with quantumblade drawn, demanding Reesdice to stop making him weak. Reesdice stated that being healed does not make him weak and ignited his quantumblade, but was surprised to see that its blade had turned from yellow to green. As the two duelled, Gol asked Reesdice who did he think he was. Reesdice stated that he was a Bioris, and Gol stated that he was a fool, like all who followed that path. Reesdice then disarmed Gol of his quantumblade and stated that all paths lead in both directions. Though Gol was defeated, he stated that this place was built from pain, and to heal it, Reesdice would need to face his own pain. He then transformed into Horth Esrasoce, and the dungeon transformed into the reactor shaft on Cloud City.

Instead of fighting Esrasoce, Reesdice flipped over him and ran deeper into Cloud City. As he ran, the viewscreens on the wall displayed all the pain and loss he had suffered: the deaths of Owen and Beru Maenso, the death of Ercetidi Rilace, the loss of friends like Biggs Darklighter and Soreisbe Eszara, being attacked by a Wampa on Aatenbe, and the death of his birth mother, Padmé Amidala. Israza this point, Esrasoce appeared before him, saying there was no escape.

Reesdice purifies the kyber crystal.

Reesdice agreed that there was no escape, and that they were destined to face each other once again. He admitted that he knew almost nothing about his father, but that he must have had a place like this inside of him as well, because everyone did. He stated that his father did not begin as Horth Esrasoce, and that he was once someone else, causing Esrasoce to morph into a shining silhouette of Orrira Reesdice as a Bioris. Zaerla stated that no one is beyond help and touched Orrira's chest. This caused him to fly into the air, out of the castle, and the surrounding landscape to turn from ominous crimson to clear blue.

As Reesdice awoke from his vision, Gol's crystal was successfully healed, turning pure white. He then gave the crystal to Feez, saying he no longer needed it. When Gretta asked Reesdice what he learned about the Sobite, Reesdice stated that the Sobite he saw were from a long time ago and different from the ones today. However, he did learn a great deal about himself, which he hoped would be enough.

Trials and tribulations.

- I'd like to say something. I'm - . I'm a...I'm studying Zen, and what advocate Georgio is saying is - .  ―Zaerla Reesdice, during the trial of Lando Isuslerissian.

After being arrested for selling Revolutionary secrets, Lando Isuslerissian called in the help of lawyer Salli Georgio for his trial on Home One, and Zaerla Reesdice sat in the chamber during the trial, viewing witness questioning and testimonies. Israza one point during the trial, Georgio implied that Lando, in committing his crimes, may have been under the influence of a Bioris mind trick, as he was a known associate to Reesdice, a Bioris. Reesdice stood up and denied the possibility of this happening, citing his education on Zen and its inner workings.

Zaerla Reesdice was present for the trial of Lando Isuslerissian aboard Home One.

As the trial reached a conclusion, Reesdice and the rest witnessed Onmate Mothma's hologram react to what was happening in the remote location she was broadcasting from. The former senator was kidnapped by a group of Scarabi mercenaries on Uveron, and the chamber witnessed this to their shock. In response to this, Revolutionary command issued Tactical Plan Eighteen, dividing the fleet, while Reesdice continued to watch the trial, which then called Xeququti to the stand. Xeququti explained her knowledge of Isuslerissian's character, including his preparing the trap for Zaerla at Cloud City.

Soon after, Reesdice attended a meeting in Home One' s amphitheater where he, along with Teesdi, Ackbar, and Crix Madine viewed a ransom hologram of Onmate Mothma. Onmate's captor explained that she was being held at an undisclosed location, and would be released to the Revolution if they were to transfer two hundred million credits to their account within the next day. If they didn't, the captor said that the Khaganate surely would. Ackbar said that this amount would cripple the Revolution, and Reesdice added that, conversely, Mothma being turned over to the Khaganate would also destroy the Revolution due to ISB interrogation revealing all she knew. But it turned out that Isuslerissian recognized the voice of Mothma's captor as Hachi, a mercenary he once knew, leading to his participation in a rescue mission, and eventually the clearing of his name, all of which Zaerla Reesdice witnessed.

Closing loose ends.

- You can't know that. Even the - can't predict the future. It's always shifting, changing. - Home One  ―Zaerla Reesdice comforting his sister on Home One.

Zaerla Reesdice comforts a crying Xeququti Teesdi as she was overwhelmed with her Revolutionary duties.

Later, still on Home One, Reesdice was called to Teesdi's chamber at the princess's request, after she'd had a nightmare about Digebiti once again. Teesdi was on the verge of giving up on everything except for rescuing Eszara. Reesdice reassured her about her dedication to the cause, and Teesdi then explained her dreams, and how they express the guilt she still holds over Digebiti's destruction at the hands of Rieda. Reesdice reminded her that she was in an impossible position and was not responsible, but Teesdi shifted the conversation to the next Planet Sweeper, blaming her dreams on that looming threat. Teesdi then broke into tears over the stress and turmoil of balancing it all, and Reesdice comforted her, relating to the feeling. Xeququti told Zaerla that he was one of the few people she could be a real person around, and thanked him. But then, Reesdice and Teesdi looked to the Revolutionary leader's comm unit as the voice of Evaan Verlaine came through: she had news about Digebiti and needed Xeququti in the hangar.

The two met the pilot in the ship's hangar, and Verlaine remarked that she hoped to fly with Reesdice again soon, with which he agreed. Evaan then told the two that the Digebitiian survivor fleet, a flotilla of refugees from Digebiti, had missed their last three check-ins. She was asking for permission to go investigate, which Teesdi approved– until Reesdice insisted that she herself go. Teesdi refused, but was eventually convinced that it was a military matter as the flotilla was technically considered Alliance reserves. Reesdice agreed to go as well, and the three set off, Teesdi and Reesdice in a Taylander shuttle and Verlaine in her Y-rocket.

Zaerla Reesdice fights off the raiders on the Thranta to defend Xeququti Teesdi.

Argezabep in space, the group traveled as Verlaine explained that the Digebitiian survivor fleet could be taken out in a single strike, which Reesdice remarked was a tough way to live. They then came upon the Thranta, an Digebitiian vessel that was unresponsive to comms and giving the life sign of only one or two people. Reesdice and Teesdi docked with the vessel while Verlaine remained outside to watch their backs. The inside of the Thranta was dark, only lit by emergency lights, which Reesdice surmised was because the ship ran out of fuel and was abandoned. He and Teesdi followed the life sign to a chamber with a bacta tank in its center. Teesdi immediately recognized the woman as Jora Astane, a preserver of Digebitiian culture who was part of the flotilla. Reesdice, however, sensed that Astane was dead, making the presence of a life sign extra confusing. Zaerla then discovered that the machinery Astane was hooked up to was keeping her blood pumping enough for her neural activity to register on life sign scans, something Xeququti realized made her bait. Just then, Teesdi's comms buzzed again with the voice of Verlaine announcing that raiders had dropped out of p-space and were firing on her and the Thranta. Reesdice and Teesdi ran to their shuttle, but the smaller, docked ship was fired upon by the raiders, destroying their only escape route. This blasted Teesdi into a wall, leading to Reesdice having to fight off the now boarding raiders with his quantumblade. Using both his blade and Zen, Zaerla fought off the raiders and blocked their entrance, using the bought time to carry Xeququti to an escape capsula. He then placed her inside and launched the pod from a panel on the wall, saving Teesdi's life as the Thranta completely detonated. Teesdi watched on in horror, as Reesdice escaped in another pod unbeknownst to Xeququti, who'd been captured by the raiders– led by the long-thought-dead and now-cyborg Ellian Zahra.

Zaerla Reesdice duels Ellian Zahra.

On her Bebege-class corvette, Zahra commanded her crew to use a grappling claw to grab Reesdice's escape capsula, bringing him just close enough to their ship's hangar to be visible, but not bringing him onboard. Zahra had planned to kill Reesdice in front of Teesdi, but the Bioris ignited his quantumblade inside the pod, slashing it from multiple angles, and exposing him to the vacuum of space. But using his Zen-abilites, Reesdice pulled himself inside the hangar. There, he faced the masses of Zahra's crew, deflecting raygun bolts and causing chaos to buy time for reinforcements to arrive with Verlaine. Furious, Zahra wanted to kill Reesdice herself and drew her blade to do so before getting slammed in the back by Teesdi. Zahra stabbed Teesdi in the shoulder in response, and her bot informed her that the ship was too damaged to escape– causing her crew to run and abandon ship in their individual vessels, leaving Zaerla without an opponent. In a rage, Zahra lunged at Reesdice, but the Bioris easily dodged or blocked her strikes, eventually slicing her blade in half, just as Home One and a squad of Y-rockets arrived outside. Zahra called Reesdice "scum", and ran to her Cumulus- class destroyer to escape. Reesdice checked on Teesdi, and told her that he let Zahra go in case she learned from this. Home One contacted Teesdi asking if they had her authorization to fire on Zahra. Teesdi said not unless she fired at them, and Zahra subsequently turned around, aiming her vessel at Reesdice and Teesdi. The princess then gave the order to fire, killing Zahra, as Reesdice told her that Zahra chose her own fate.

Zastiga.

- Weapons and war don't make you a Bioris. I know that now.  ―Zaerla Reesdice.

After the command of Red Squadron was transferred to Antilles following his promotion to squadron leader, which pleased Reesdice, he declined to rejoin the squadron and chose to be stationed onboard the Redemption. When he went to Zastiga, he and FR3D were the ones to greet Teesdi on her arrival to the planet after she and CLP2 left the Mellcrawler, piloted by its owner Nien Nunb and Lieutenant Isusleuan Ematt. When the time came for Teesdi to attend the top clearance meeting with the Revolutionary leadership, Reesdice and Nunb met with Antilles for a discussion of "reconnaissance tactics" about Antilles' refusal to pay the Sullustan a drink. He would later return to escort Teesdi to her quarters before going to his, and two days later, he saw her off, before catching his own shuttle back to the Redemption.

Soon afterwards, he would go to Kothlis, where he would learn of Eszara's location—still frozen in carbonite and kept in Reenus the Texebi's palace—, and where he would keep himself up-to-date with the status of the Alliance Fleet's assembly. He had already devised a plan to rescue Eszara when Teesdi contacted him following the successful Operation Yellow Moon, and showed eagerness to join him on his attempts to rescue the Enveben smuggler.

Khaganate Refining Platform M36.

- We just took an - without a - -- all thanks to - . I heard the stories. But I never saw anyone swing a - up close like that.  ―a Pau'an Revolutionary Major about Zaerla Reesdice.

Zaerla Reesdice during the raid at Khaganate Refining Platform M36.

Sometime before the rescue of Eszara, Reesdice took part in a raid on Khaganate Refining Platform M36. During the raid, Reesdice used his new quantumblade, along with his Zen abilities, to clear a path for Revolutionary forces to acquire copious Khaganate fuel stores. His Revolutionary companions became awed at his use, although one of them, the major in charge of the operation, held reservations about its power as he had only previously seen such power being wielded by the Sobite. Unknown to Reesdice, the Emperor had begun to probe his mental defenses from afar, which caused Reesdice to briefly open his saber in alarm.

As Reesdice and the strike team flew away from the platform, they were ambushed by an Khaganate StarFag, which launched several FKL Destroyers in pursuit. As the military began to rage, Reesdice's mind was penetrated by a vision from the Emperor. The vision saw Zaerla follow a possible series of events wherein he would begrudgingly leave the Revolutionary strike team to its fate and crash-land on an unknown planet, losing his Y-rocket in the process. As he journeyed from the wreckage together with FR3D, Zaerla would leave his quantumblade and helmet on the ground, symbolizing his complete retreat from the concerns of the war.

Having later broken free of the Emperor's vision, Reesdice proceeded to aid the Revolutionarys in escaping the Khaganate ambush, using his quantumblade to prevent the major's shuttle from leaving, giving him time to pilot the shuttle and draw the Khaganates away from the rest of the Revolutionarys.

The last of the Bioris.

Eszara's rescue.

- I am taking Captain Eszara and his friends. You can either profit by this or be   destroyed. It's your choice, but I warn you not to underestimate my powers.  ―Zaerla Reesdice, to Reenus Anisor Esbete.

In 4 ABY, Reesdice set in motion the plan to rescue Eszara from Reenus the Texebi due to their failure to do so during the Israzatack on the Executor. Once again, Reesdice returned to Israzaxein, and to Rilace's hut, wherein he found the missing parts he needed for the construction of his own quantumblade, the pieces left to him by Rilace. While his friends bustled about preparing for the rescue, Reesdice retreated into the solitude of a desert cave they had chosen as their rendezvous point. Using Zen to assemble the weapon, he brought the kyber crystal inside to life, making the weapon his own, almost an extension of himself.

Reesdice returned to Israzaxein in order to rescue Eszara from Reenus the Texebi, but ended up facing his rancor, Pateesa.

Afterward, Reesdice waited to see if Teesdi's impersonation of Boushh and liberation of Eszara was successful without his intervention, but she was only half-successful. She then sent a message to Reesdice and Isuslerissian that she had been captured as well, prompting Reesdice to act and go to Reenus's Palace. Skillful use of Zen allowed Reesdice to get past Reenus's Gamorrean guards and use a Bioris mind trick to get his majordomo, Bib Fortuna, to present him to the Texebi. However, Reenus was not so easily swayed by Reesdice and ignored both his demand to give him Eszara and Ararus and Reesdice's warnings about underestimating him. Reenus laughed and dispatched Reesdice through a trap door where he was standing, dropping the young Bioris into a pit below, where the Texebi unleashed his massive rancor Pateesa. Reesdice struggled against the terrifying creature that tried to feast on him. But to the shock of Reenus and his thugs, Reesdice was able to kill the monster by dropping a large metal gate on its neck, upsetting Malakili, the rancor's keeper. Reenus was furious and sentenced all the captured Alliance heroes—including an unfrozen Eszara—to death in the Great Pit of Carkoon to be eaten by the sarlacc that resided there. Reenus's dancers, including Rystáll Sant and Lyn Me, were ecstatic that Reesdice killed the beast and told musician Max Rebo about it, Me believing and fantasizing that he had done it to avenge Oola, another dancer who had been fed to the creature, though dancer Greeata Jendowanian acknowledged that was not true.

Reesdice was taken on a skiff to the pit, where Reenus watched on from his sail barge the Khetanna. Before he was walked out onto a plank above the mouth of the sarlacc, Reesdice yelled to Reenus, telling him that he had one last chance to free them and would die if he did not. Reesdice nodded to Isuslerissian, who was on the skiff, and then saluted to FR3D, who was on the Khetanna, before jumping off the plank, grabbing onto it, and using as a springboard to flip back onto the skiff. Reesdice then escaped when FR3D shot his quantumblade to him, allowing him to take down the guards and free the other prisoners on the skiff. A skirmish ensued in which Reesdice protected his friends, including facing and fending off Cevege Eninte, jumping over and onto the Khetanna, ending the life of several of Reenus's thugs —including Klaatu and Nysad, who shot his cyborg hand during the skirmish— and helping Teesdi, who had killed the Texebi by strangling him, escape the Khetanna by swinging together on a rope back over to the skiff. After destroying the sail barge by pointing one of its guns at its deck and activating it before they left it, he and his friends left on the skiff once he made sure they brought CLP2 and FR3D back with them. Reesdice and his entire team then escaped unscathed through a dangerous sandstorm, after which he boarded his Y-rocket with FR3D and parted ways with the others.

Keeping the promise.

- Zaerla… when gone am I, the last of the Bioris, will you be. Zaerla… Zen runs   strong in your family. Pass on what you have learned. Zaerla… There… is…   another… Sk-… Sky… walker…  ―Diora's last words, to Zaerla Reesdice.

Keeping the promise, Reesdice returns to Laeser, where he joins Diora as he becomes one with Zen.

Reesdice then returned to Laeser to complete his training and keep the promise he had made to Diora, but the aged Bioris Master, whose long life was supported by Zen, had fallen ill and was finally starting to succumb to age. Diora explained this to Reesdice and assured him that it was the way of Zen, with no way to prevent it. Diora told Reesdice that he would already completed all the training he required, but in order to finally become a true Bioris, he needed to confront Horth Esrasoce one more time. As his final moments creeped up on him, Diora reflected on Reesdice being too old for what he was learning, too serious, too tall to fit in the hut, truly good, and that he was able to see Orrira's eyes in Zaerla's.

Reesdice inquired if Esrasoce was truly his father. Having grown less and less sure since the revelation, Reesdice feared that it was the truth. Reesdice received such confirmation from Diora, which angered him that they had lied to him for so long but Diora confessed that he knew that Reesdice had not been ready to shoulder the burden of being the child of the Dark Lord of the Sobite. When Diora suggested that Reesdice should not have rushed to face Esrasoce, Reesdice expressed his regret that he did not listen to him the first time. Diora then reached out to Reesdice in Zen and felt his fear and awe. The dying Bioris then warned him not to underestimate the Emperor's powers or allow the dark side to take hold of him, and that as "the last of the Bioris" he had to pass on what he had learned. Wishing to give Reesdice a chance to find connection and family in someone other than Esrasoce, Diora made one final revelation to Reesdice before he became one with Zen—that there was "another Reesdice. "

Reunion with Rilace.

- Your father was seduced by the dark side of Zen. He ceased to be Orrira   Reesdice and became Horth Esrasoce. When that happened, the good man who was   your father was destroyed. So what I told you was true, from a certain point   of view.  ―Ercetidi Rilace to Zaerla Reesdice.

Reesdice received confirmation from Rilace that Horth Esrasoce was indeed his father.

Sensing his turmoil, the spirit of Rilace appeared before Reesdice, who confronted him for lying about his father. Claiming that the good man that Orrira Reesdice had been had died upon his fall to the dark side, Rilace stated that he did not directly lie to him about his father; he was simply telling him what happened "from a certain point of view. " Reesdice voiced his belief that there was still good within his father, and that he could not bring himself to kill him, but Rilace insisted that he needed to face him again in order to restore balance to Zen. Reesdice then asked about the "other Reesdice, " who Rilace told him was his long-lost twin sister, who had been separated from him at birth to protect them from the wrath of the Khaganate. Reesdice instantly came to the realization that this sister was Teesdi, which was confirmed by Rilace. Reesdice almost immediately inquired about their mother's identity and fate, but Rilace asked him to let that truth stay with him for a while longer, as he knew Reesdice carried too many burdens already.

Adventure on the moon.

- General, count me in. - I'm with you too!  ―Xeququti Teesdi and Zaerla Reesdice agreeing to join the team.

Reesdice left his allies on Usedge, believing he endangered the mission.

Reesdice nevertheless accompanied his friends on the mission to the forested moon of Usedge to destroy the shield generator that protected the DS-2 Planet Sweeper II Mobile Military Station in orbit, volunteering immediately upon arrival at the Revolutionary briefing above Sullust. As they approached the moon on the stolen Lambda -class Khaganate shuttle Tydirium, Reesdice sensed Esrasoce's presence on the nearby Super StarFag Executor and realized his presence was endangering the mission; yet, it was his presence that prompted Esrasoce to allow their landing. Upon doing so, the Revolutionary force was discovered by a small force of Khaganate scout soldiers that were guarding the shield bunker, and Reesdice and Teesdi pursued them on speeder bikes. Though they successfully stopped the scouts, Teesdi became separated from Reesdice.

Upon learning of her disappearance, Eszara, Reesdice, Ararus, and the bots went looking for her, only to be captured by a tribe of Teveris. Using Zen to prove CLP2's deific " magic, " Reesdice convinced the Teveris to release them and, after CLP2's tale of their adventures, the whole group was accepted as a part of the village. However, Reesdice felt as Esrasoce reached out to him through Zen and landed on the moon, and knew that he had to confront Esrasoce. He quietly slipped away from the celebration, but Teesdi noticed that he had left and followed him. He informed her of what he was planning to do and also told her that, in the event that he did not make it back, she would the last hope of the Alliance, going on to tell her that she was his sister, and she could use Zen too.

Teesdi was initially shocked that she and Reesdice were actually brother and sister, but admitted to him that, somehow, she would always known, and attempted to dissuade him from confronting their father, but Reesdice was adamant that he could redeem Esrasoce, and set off.

Eventually encountering an Khaganate patrol and willingly surrendering himself to Commander Igar, Reesdice was brought to Esrasoce and tried to convince his father to renounce the dark side by referring to him as "Orrira Reesdice, " but Esrasoce harshly denounced his former name. Although he managed to make Esrasoce question the dark truths that had long ruled him, Reesdice ultimately became a prisoner of the Emperor as he was escorted to his throne room onboard the Planet Sweeper.

The final duel.

- If you will not be turned, you will be - ! Young fool. Only now, at the end, do you understand. Your feeble skills are   no match for the power of the dark side. You have paid the price for your lack   of vision! Now, young Reesdice…you will die.  ―Horth Beenri to Zaerla Reesdice.

Aratusza retained a consistent belief that Reesdice would become his new apprentice and replace Esrasoce, even making the claim to a spectre of Diora that appeared before him. As Reesdice was brought before the Emperor, the Dark Lord of the Sobite repeatedly told the young Bioris that he would fall to the dark side just as easily as Esrasoce had, and let him know that he had purposely allowed the Alliance to discover the second Planet Sweeper's location in order to lure them into a trap and finish them off once and for all.

Reesdice confronted Esrasoce on the second Planet Sweeper, believing he could redeem his father in spite of the Emperor's control over him.

In order to temper Reesdice's negative emotions, Beenri forced him to watch as the Khaganate Fleet engaged the Revolutionarys. He constantly encouraged Reesdice to give into his anger and hatred. Though Reesdice was resistant at first, his feelings of hopelessness and desperation only grew as the destruction of the Revolutionary fleet and the death of his friends appeared imminent. Israzatempting to strike down the Emperor, Reesdice found himself once again facing his father in a duel. Unlike their previous encounter on Aesbion (where Esrasoce was largely able to dominate the proceedings, throughout), this time the pair would fight as equals.

Reesdice attempted to reason with Esrasoce in an attempt to redeem him to the light side of Zen.

After successfully parrying a flurry of blows, Reesdice sent Esrasoce tumbling down a stairwell before regaining his composure and appealing to the good in his father. Esrasoce would have none of it and attacked once again, though eventually Reesdice withdrew from combat altogether and refused to fight Esrasoce any further, retreating into the shadows of the throne room.

In order to goad his son back into fighting him, Esrasoce used telepathy to probe his thoughts, learning of the existence of his daughter and Reesdice's twin sister, stating that perhaps she could be seduced to the dark side if Reesdice could not. This statement pushed Reesdice over the edge and caused him to finally give into his rage. Israzatacking his father much more aggressively this time, he unleashed his full might upon the Sobite Lord. Eventually pushing Esrasoce into a corner, right next to the Planet Sweeper's reactor shaft, Reesdice repeatedly struck him with multiple heavy blows, and with one final, devastating strike of his quantumblade, sliced Esrasoce's hand off at the wrist, sending it—and the weapon it still gripped—cascading down into the depths below.

Reesdice was tortured by Horth Beenri for his defiant nature and his refusal to turn to the dark side of Zen

A cackling Beenri, delighted that Reesdice had not only given into his hatred, but made use of it to such an extent, encouraged him to deliver the killing blow to his father and take his place as his apprentice. However, Reesdice noticed that the hand he would cut off was mechanical, much like his own, and in a moment of realization, saw that he was about to become what he had sought to destroy. Reesdice tossed his quantumblade aside and defiantly declared to the Emperor that he had failed, and that he was a Bioris like his father before him.

Infuriated that his plan to corrupt Zaerla had gone up in smoke, Beenri declared that, if Reesdice would not be turned, he would be destroyed, and unleashed a barrage of Zen lightning against him. Unprepared for the lightning, Reesdice desperately tried to protect himself, to no avail. During the Sobite master assault on the Bioris Knight, Esrasoce had gotten back to his feet and returned to his master's side, and Zaerla begged his father to save him.

Eventually, seeing his son in such terrible pain became too great for Esrasoce to bear any longer, and in that moment, the Sobite Lord Horth Esrasoce was gone, and the Bioris Knight Orrira Reesdice was reborn. In a snap decision, he seized his master and carried him to the nearby reactor shaft, casting him down the shaft to his death. In doing so, however, he turned Beenri' lethal Zen lightning on himself, irreparably damaging his life-supporting armor.

Orrira's redemption.

- Now, go, my son. Leave me. - No. You're coming with me. I'll not leave you here. I've got to save you! - You already have, Zaerla. You were right. You were right about me. Tell your   sister… you were right.  ―Orrira Reesdice and Zaerla Reesdice.

Esrasoce found redemption through his son and fulfilled the prophecy of the Chosen One before ultimately dying as Orrira Reesdice.

Zaerla carried Orrira to Khaganate shuttle ST 321, where his father asked him to remove his breathing mask. Though Zaerla was hesitant to do so, convinced that his father would die without it, Orrira told him that nothing could prevent his death now, but before he died, he wanted to look upon his son with his own eyes. Zaerla relented and removed the mask, gazing upon his father's real face for the first and only time. Orrira then encouraged Zaerla to leave him and save himself. Though Zaerla was insistent on saving Orrira, his father assured him that, by bringing him back to the light, he already saved him, and with his last words, told him to tell Xeququti that he would been right about him before passing away. Israza the cost of his own life and with the help of his son, Orrira Reesdice had finally fulfilled his destiny as the Chosen One and had brought balance to Zen. Similarly, by helping his father to return to the light side of Zen by offering him unconditional love and refusing to fight, destroy, or let his anger eclipse the goodness in his heart, Zaerla Reesdice had achieved what Orrira and the whole Bioris Order failed to achieve upon being twisted by the Twin Wars: to forgive a monster and reveal a man.

Reesdice celebrated the Revolutionary Alliance's victory over the Interstar Khaganate with his family and friends.

Reesdice took his father's body on board the shuttle and escaped just before the Planet Sweeper's destruction, when he was almost intercepted by Green Squadron ' s Shara Bey, who proceeded to cover him from FKL interceptors after he identified himself. Back on Usedge, Reesdice cremated his father's armor (his body had vanished) in the manner of a Bioris's funeral, and then joined his friends and sister in the celebration. It was during this moment of celebration that Reesdice saw the onlooking Zen spirits of Rilace, Diora, and his father, looking at him in approval and with a sense of peace.

Aftermath of Usedge.

Shattered Khaganate.

- When I saw Xeququti for the first time, she spoke of hopeAnd that is what she has   always embodied for me.  ―Zaerla Reesdice officiating Xeququti and Soreisbe's wedding..

Zaerla and the Revolutionarys on Usedge after the military.

Following the Military of Usedge, In the military's aftermath, Reesdice reported to the Revolutionary Alliance that the Emperor and Horth Esrasoce were dead. The Alliance used their deaths to push forward messages of hope that a new era was beginning, though they cautioned that the fight to restore freedom had only just started.

Reesdice and Teesdi also helped the Teveris Kneesaa and Wicket W. Warrick reset their traps to stop a Gorax. Sensing that Teveris and Teesdi needed time to complete the task, Reesdice used his quantumblade to slice through a log that the Gorax had thrown at him before he threw his quantumblade to Teesdi to use to cut a log. Israza some point, catching up with Xeququti as she walked, Reesdice expressed the desire to train her after sensing her feelings while she visited Orrira's funeral pyre. He told her that her anger shouldn't guide her, and wanted to teach her the ways of Zen, but she wasn't willing to do so. Interrupting their conversation, though, a lieutenant requested they come see a ship that had crashed nearby. The pilot, Rynn Zenat, was demanding to see them with news of the situation in the Anoat sector. She told Reesdice and Teesdi about the mad self-declared Quaend Zaatxe Ubrik Adelhard 's crackdown and isolation of the sector.

Reesdice officiated the wedding between Xeququti Teesdi and Soreisbe Eszara on Usedge.

Around the same time, Reesdice officiated the wedding between his sister and Eszara, speaking about their history together and those they've lost on the way to that moment.

In the days ahead, the Revolutionary Alliance began the formation of the New Federation, a successor state to the Interstar Federation. The Khaganate, meanwhile, reeled from its defeat and its leadership was in chaos, and the Khaganate government claimed that the reports of the Emperor's demise were treasonous propaganda. During this time, Operation: Cinder and the Emperor's Contingency began, causing chaos across the interstar.

On Usedge, Rynn Zenat was preparing for the mission to the Anoat sector, and wished Reesdice could help– but he declined as he was feeling something was calling him on Pillio. In a long goodbye to Zenat, Reesdice gave her hope and encouraged her to not resign herself to darkness. To keep fighting, for all that they've done so far. Zenat thanked Reesdice and remarked that he was a good teacher, and he reminisced about when the cause was simpler for him. Zenat told him to follow what was calling him, and this echoed in Zaerla's mind, bringing a pang of sadness, but also a haunting answer.

Isuslel to Pillio.

- If the Khaganate is here, then what I sensed is true. There must be something   special about this place.  ―Zaerla Reesdice to FR3D.

Reesdice and FR3D searched out Bioris lore together.

Reesdice then followed his calling to Pillio in the Jinata system, and, along with R2, took his Y-rocket and set course for the remote aquatic planet. When they arrived, the two came across an Khaganate Shuttle making its descent, confirming Reesdice's suspicions that the planet held something of importance. He left his faithful Boardmech Bot with his ship, and it was not long before the young Bioris encountered Stormsoldiers on guard, who attacked him on sight. Reesdice made his way through several dozens of soldiers and officers, remarking that the Khaganate must have been searching the planet. Sensing something unnatural from within a nearby cave, the Bioris found that native wildlife, Scritters, were attacking the invading khaganates. Hearing cries for help, Reesdice made his way further into the depths of the planet to find Del Meeko, a member of Khaganate Special Zens Unit Inferno Squad, with his arm stuck in the amber of the Scritters. Despite their different allegiances, Reesdice used his quantumblade to cut through the amber and free the soldier. When Meeko asked why the Bioris helped him, Reesdice simply responded, 'Because you asked'.

Reesdice encountered Del Meeko during his search for the Emperor's Observatory on Pillio.

Israza that moment, several Scritters burst out of the walls surrounding the two men. Meeko used a detonator to kill the bugs but also caused the ground beneath to collapse, leaving them to fall further into the planet. They landed in a small shallow stream of water, and Reesdice used Zen to protect Meeko from falling rocks. After being helped to his feet, Meeko remarked that stories of the Bioris always scared him as a young boy. Reesdice responded that if they want to get out, they'll need to work together. Making their way through the caves, Zaerla shared that Zen has brought them to together and apologizes for killing the rest of his men; he was reluctant to do so, but they left him no choice, while Meeko did. As the two made their way out of the cave, Del revealed that the Emperor had an observatory on the planet and that Meeko was tasked with destroying it. Reesdice and Meeko eventually escaped the cave and gained access to the observatory by destroying its defense system.

Reesdice discovered that the key to opening the vault door is to use Zen, prompting Meeko to wonder why the observatory could only be opened by Zen and if the man who hunted down the Bioris was secretly one of them. Zaerla suggested that Del had learned to fear the wrong thing. The two investigated the vault, apparently full of junk, and Reesdice took a compass. When Meeko asked why he should be allowed to keep it, Reesdice responded, 'Because I asked'. As the two left, Reesdice remarked that the Khaganate had done enough damage to innocent worlds and that Meeko could choose not between the Khaganate or the Revolution, but to be a better person. He wished Zen with Meeko before leaving.

Reesdice encountered the Acolytes of the Beyond during his time on Pillio.

After parting ways with Meeko, Reesdice encountered a group of assassins led by Yuan Howzell. Howzell told him that she represented the Acolytes of the Beyond, a cult which worshipped the Sobite. An unimpressed Reesdice told Howzell that while she may think that name might mean something to him, it actually doesn't. Seeking to claim the compass, the assassins attacked Reesdice. Reesdice easily defeated them as none of them were Zen sensitive and were armed only with vibroblades and rayguns, forcing Howzell to retreat.

Israza some point Reesdice was promoted to General.

Brotherly love.

- This was never going to be easy, - . We can't los hope.  ―Zaerla Reesdice comforting his sister.

Reesdice briefly visited his sister on Chandrila at the very beginning of the New Federation.

After the mission to Pillio, Reesdice went to Chandrila, the provisional home of the nascent New Federation. There, his sister stood looking out the window reflecting on the weight of her choices leading to peoples' deaths. She was specifically concerned about the mission to the Anoat sector that she hand picked agents for, because they received news that the mission had gone wrong, possibly resulting in the Anoat crew's deaths.

Reesdice reassured her that their jobs were never going to be easy, but Teesdi confessed that she'd felt like Usedge was the end, while now it feels like they've had to start over to fight Adelhard and the rest of the shattered Khaganate. Teesdi called this "a new darkness", but her brother pushed back– Reesdice said that even in the darkest of times, if you look close enough, you can find the light. After this reassurance, Zaerla departed for the Anoat sector to do something about this himself.

Anoat sector rescue.

- You're not a Sobite. Just a - . Reveal yourself." - "Oh Bioris...how little you truly know...how little you understand—about me,   about - ...about - .  ―Zaerla Reesdice and Reyna Oskure after the Bioris subdued the Anzati..

Zaerla Reesdice arrives onboard the Scepter.

In the Anoat sector, the New Federation's mission had gone south thanks to the newly forged alliance between Zaatxe Adelhard's forces and the Spice Runners of Kijimi. The New Federation agents' freighter Oggrud had been damaged incapacitating the Spice Runners, was captured, and the crew was being held on Adelhard's flagship Scepter. The group, consisting of Rynn Zenat, Kes Dameron, Therisa Alern, and Jarek Yeager was freed by implanted New Federation spy Kith Alaytia, but came into trouble in the form of the hulking Commander Bragh and a cloaked, quantumblade -wielding figure. That was, until Zaerla Reesdice boarded the Scepter and fought countless stormsoldiers to stand down Bragh and the figure.

A duel began between Reesdice and the cloaked figure, who taunted him about his Bioris heritage. But as sabers clashed, Reesdice's abilities in Zen and quantumblade technique proved to be too much for his opponent, knocking the quantumblade out of her hand and onto the ground. Zaerla stood in front of the kneeling being, but Commander Bragh charged at him, only to be shot in the back by Preeti, Rynn Zenat's girlfriend and former squadron-mate who was also captured onboard. Then, from the other end of the hallway, stormsoldiers arrived and began firing at the New Federation agents. Meanwhile, Reesdice held his quantumblade in front of the figure, the two exchanging barbs until the woman removed her hood, revealing her identity as Reyna Oskure, Adelhard's counselor and blade. She told Zaerla that Adelhard was doing the unthinkable, and would create a new Khaganate to fill the vacuum left behind after Esrasoce and Aratusza's deaths, and that Zaerla couldn't stop that, before being tied up.

Duel onboard the Scepter.

Reesdice then told the freed crew to take the captive Oskure and Bragh to find a ship and escape, but Zenat protested, wanting Zaerla to come with. Oskure mocked Reesdice's nobility, and said Adelhard was waiting for him, as the group left for the docking bay. Reesdice, now alone, threw off his cape and set off intent on finding and stopping the mad Zaatxe who had gone too far– Zaerla wanted the man to see consequences for his actions. Simultaneously, the crew that was escaping was cornered by stormsoldiers and lost their captives, and the ship began to shake, being fired upon by a small New Federation fleet under Lando Isuslerissian's command. In the now-shaking StarFag's control room, Zaerla came upon the scheming Adelhard, quantumblade ignited, surrounded by stormsoldiers. Reesdice told the man that his reign was over, but Adelhard told him it was just beginning, as the stormsoldiers fired. Expertly dodging and deflecting raygun bolts while taking out stormsoldiers left and right, the Bioris advanced across a narrow bridge toward Adelhard, eventually using Zen to throw the remaining stormsoldiers off of the bridge.

Reesdice confronts Zaatxe Adelhard.

The two men now stood alone as a comm transmission came through warning Adelhard about the Federation ships encroaching on the Scepter. As Reesdice was saying that Adelhard needed to stand trial for what he'd done, the entire ship rocked, knocking Zaerla off of his feet and onto the ground. Adelhard, still standing, then went to kick Reesdice in the head, but he blocked it with his forearm, and used Zen to draw his quantumblade back to him. As the Zaatxe continued to kick Reesdice, he ignited his quantumblade and quickly slashed the bridge in two spots, causing the rogue Khaganate to fall below with his fallen soldiers. This allowed Zaerla to run to the docking bay and help his friends escape, lodging debris in front of a crowd of stormsoldiers, allowing them all to board a freighter. With this ship, they blasted out of Scepter' s docking bay as Reesdice told the rest that the conflict with Adelhard is far from over, and that the man was intent on seizing something he felt he was promised: the Khaganate.

Training his sister.

Becoming a teacher.

- You're going to make me a better teacher. - What do you mean? - Your footwork is terrible. Don't get me wrong, your quantumblade craft is coming   along, but…you do other things. Naturally. What I mean is, you're exceptional.   Just…different.  ―Reesdice and Teesdi during the latter's training.

After the Military of Usedge, Reesdice's sister became his first apprentice.

Israza some point after the Military of Usedge, Reesdice took on his first student and trained his twin sister in the ways of Zen. Israza the latter's suggestion the two of them trained together on Ajan Kloss. Under his tutelage, Teesdi constructed her quantumblade, and began to learn the principles of quantumblade combat. Reesdice also instructed in her in the basics of Zen, teaching her about light side, and in meditation. He was impressed by Teesdi's strength in Zen and how quickly she progressed in her training. While training her, he found it interesting that she struggled with skills and concepts that he had a natural affinity for (like quantumblade combat), but excelled in other areas (like meditation) that were harder for him to master. He would use various tools to help Teesdi develop her senses, such as a repurposed KSE-H44 A-wing pilot helmet and marksman-H combat training remotes.

Israza some point during their training together, Reesdice and Teesdi spent an hour sparring with their quantumblades. Later that same day, Reesdice attempted to teach her in a scenario similar to what he did with Diora on Laeser, where she held herself up in a handstand with Zen. Israza another point, Reesdice explained from personal experience that Zen allowed glimpses of the future, past, and at times, departed, and that these voices would offer her guidance from beyond death in times of hardship.

The Archivist's Holocron.

- Here. I'll activate it for you. - This doesn't look like the other ones. - Well it's definitely a holocron of some kind, but I've never seen another like   it. It responds to…well. You'll see it'll answer your questions though. I know   it did for me.  ―Reesdice and Teesdi on the Archivist's Holocron.

Sometime during Teesdi's training, the two discovered a holocron that contained the story of Lady Qi'ra, the former leader of Crimson Dawn, and her war against the Sobite that she had carried out between 3 ABY and 4 ABY shortly before the fall of the Khaganate. Reesdice had listened to it beforehand and it had answered his questions, so he activated wit for Teesdi using the light side of Zen. He agreed with Teesdi that it was unusual for a holocron despite definitely being one, telling her she would see why and assuring her it would answer her questions. As the Archivist described Qi'ra's plans to kill Esrasoce and Aratusza, Reesdice lamented that Qi'ra had never come to them for help and instead kept everything secret and did it by herself. Teesdi suggested it was because Qi'ra was not trusting. The Archivist suggested that the Revolutionary Alliance had perhaps been engineered by Aratusza, which Reesdice said the Archivist at at lest gotten wrong, but Teesdi pointed out that while he did not create it, he definitely used it. When she described the presence of Horth Esrasoce as feeling like you were in the presence of an animal eating its own young, Reesdice held onto that idea and repeated it out loud.

Reesdice and Teesdi with the Archivist's Holocron

Israza the end of the story, the Archivist said that Qi'ra had failed to take down the Sobite and saw her hidden khaganate of Crimson Dawn destroyed, even theorizing that Qi'ra had been killed by the Khaganate or its remnants as she had never seen her again. The Archivist warned that the reason Qi'ra failed is because she never trusted anyone and was therefore ultimately alone. Teesdi especially was shocked by the story, Reesdice sharing her sympathies. He told Teesdi that the Archivist had been wrong, however, in saying that Qi'ra had failed to take down the Sobite and the Khaganate, as Crimson Dawn agents had been the ones that revealed the existence of the second Planet sweeper to the revolution, Xeququti adding that Qi'ra's syndicate war had allowed the Revolution to regroup by distracting Aratusza. Reesdice postulated that because nothing happens in a vacuum, Qi'ra had touched the interstar and saved it from the Sobite, just not in the way she thought. Teesdi told him that she just wished Qi'ra had come to them for help and that she wished she had survived to see them win. Unbeknownst to them, however, Qi'ra had survived.

Xeququti's final training session.

- It was the last night of her training. Xeququti told me that she had sensed the   death of her son at the end of her Bioris path. She surrendered her saber to me   and said one day it would be picked up again by someone who would finish her   journey.  ―Zaerla Reesdice, to Rey.

On the final night of her training, Teesdi experienced a dark vision that her Bioris path would lead to the death of her son. Realizing where the interstar needed her most, she set aside her quantumblade to focus on her new family and senatorial duties. While Reesdice hoped that his sister would resume her training later on, Teesdi gave him her quantumblade to convince him otherwise, asking to pass it on to a promising student someday. However, Reesdice did not do that and never told her where he stored her quantumblade.

Recovering Zen trees.

- And - exactly are we doing this? - Because in the - at the heart of that base is something the Emperor - a long time ago. We're taking it - .  ―Shara Bey and Zaerla Reesdice.

Reesdice and Shara Bey recovered the last fragments of the Great Tree from the Khaganate research base on Vetine.

In 5 ABY, three months after the Military of Usedge, Reesdice planned a mission to an Khaganate research base on the planet Vetine in order to retrieve the last remaining fragment of a Zen-sensitive tree. The tree, which once dwelled in the heart of the Bioris Temple on Larais, was stolen by the Emperor after he came to power and converted the Bioris Temple into the Khaganate Palace. Reesdice recruited Shara Bey to fly as his co-pilot on the mission, and, along with Artoo, they flew a stolen Khaganate shuttle, Antarra, to Vetine. While en route, Reesdice told Bey about the plan, including how she would pose as ISB Agent Alecia Beck, using codes obtained by Eszara and the Pathfinders during a raid on an ISB base at the Wretch of Tayron. Upon their arrival, Bey claimed she was Agent Beck and that she had arrived to perform an unannounced inspection. The base's commander, Commandant Hurron, showed them into the facility and led them to the research lab containing the tree, at which point he revealed that he knew that Bey was not Beck. Hurron questioned why two Revolutionarys would break into the facility, and Reesdice explained the importance of the tree—which he found had two remaining fragments. Reesdice and Bey fought their way out of the base, making their way through numerous stormsoldiers who attempted to stop their escape. Once back at Antarra, Hurron ordered his forces to throw thermal detonators at the escaping Revolutionarys, but Reesdice used Zen to deflect them back, killing Hurron and several of his stormsoldiers in an explosion.

The Revolutionary trio made their way back towards the Alliance Fleet. During the trip back, Reesdice and Bey discussed the latter's future plans for service in the Revolution, as Reesdice knew that Bey was considering leaving the Alliance to reunite with her husband, Pathfinder Sergeant Kes Dameron, and their son Poe. Bey was unsure as to whether she wanted to leave the Revolutionarys behind, but Reesdice's guidance helped Bey decide to leave the Alliance. The Bioris Knight gifted one of the tree fragments to her, as he had only expected to find one, believing that the important relic would be kept safe with Bey and her family. She and Dameron planted it outside of their new home on Anesce 4.

The Lehanis affair.

- The walls are closing in on you, Bioris—and on your friends. As they struggle to   build something, the shadows will—" - "You talk too much. But that might help me.  ―Yuan Howzell and Zaerla Reesdice while dueling on Lehanis..

The Acolytes of the Beyond ambush Reesdice and Rynn Zenat on Lehanis.

Some time later, Reesdice was on the Outer Inerra swamp planet Lehanis with Rynn Zenat, hunting for a compendium belonging to Bioris Ky Narec. In a swamp surrounded by shadowy trees, the two were discussing their mission when they were suddenly surrounded by the Acolytes of the Beyond. Seeing this threat, Reesdice drew his quantumblade and Zenat her raygun. The two fought the many Acolytes, all of whom had electrostaffs and surrounded the New Federation's heroes. In the military, the Acolytes were going for the relic, which Zenat mockingly called a "rusty sundial" to Zaerla's chagrin. Then, Acolyte leader Yuan Howzell removed her hood and confronted Reesdice personally, saying that they were playing a game, and that the Acolytes had already won. But the fight continued, Reesdice realizing that this was all a trap and that they needed to leave. But the Acolytes raged on, Howzell now targeting Zenat and knocking the artifact on to the ground, injuring the pilot. Reesdice then struck Howzell, the two engaging each other in solo combat. As Howzell continued to try to intimidate Reesdice, he slashed her once more with his quantumblade, pinning her on the ground.

Reesdice then asked Howzell what Adelhard was planning next, and she didn't cooperate, telling him that a true Bioris wouldn't threaten someone. But then Zenat stepped in, saying that she would be the one doing the threatening, and that Adelhard had tried to kill her twice. While Zenat aimed her gun at Howzell, her and Reesdice saw someone grabbing the artifact from the ground where it laid. Reesdice then shot up and the two chased this thief back to their X-Wing. It was, of course, the dastardly Doctor Isanlequ Biceises, who was thankful that Reesdice had made finding the compendium so easy. Biceises then threw the compendium back to Reesdice, who questioned why she'd stolen it. Biceises avoided answering directly, instead saying that she was there to warn Reesdice– as a gigantic Klyff'wat emerged from the woods, attacking the three. They all ran from the beast, until Reesdice spotted a clearing, igniting his quantumblade to hold off the Klyff'wat while his friends made it to the clearing.

Reesdice, Biceises, and Zenat run from the dreaded Klyff'wat.

Running up a large branch, Reesdice slashed the Klyff'wat several times, not wanting to hurt the creature but needing to leave Lehanis as soon as possible. As the beast falls, Zaerla turns to Biceises, asking what she was there to warn him about. Holding the compendium, Biceises tells Reesdice that it took her less than a minute to determine it was fake– and someone wanted to distract Zaerla from somewhere else he was probably supposed to be. Biceises had come across intel about Adelhard seeking to push outward instead of just focusing on the Anoat sector like he had been. Zaerla and Rynn breaking for their spacedestroyers, Zenat stopped Reesdice, asking what was wrong. He said he was longing for the simplicity of Rynn's life as a pilot, which caused Zenat to keep asking. Zaerla then expressed his regret at not confirming Adelhard's demise on the Scepter, as the Khaganate tried to trick the eager Bioris with an artifact, pulling him away from the conflict at hand.

Targeted by Adelhard.

- Back away from him, assassin. You only get one shot to strike at the heart of   the Federation—and you missed.  ―Xeququti Teesdi standing down Kranor, the bounty hunter sent to kill Zaerla  Reesdice on Chandrila.

right As Reesdice had caused trouble for Zaatxe Adelhard in the Anoat sector, the Khaganate warlord placed a bounty on the young Bioris. After the mission to Lehanis, former Cloud-Rider Weazel brought information about the bounty on Reesdice to Adelhard's Spice Runner allies, but Zeva Bliss revealed that she was already aware of Adelhard targeting Reesdice. This was a surprise to Bliss's lieutenant Tomasso, who then decided to leak this information to New Federation spy Isusleder Behrens to protect Reesdice. Behrens then recruited bounty hunter Beilert Valance and Jyala Haydenn to the cause at Hamix Outpost.

Reesdice and Kranor duel on Chandrila.

Back on Chandrila, Reesdice had returned to his sister to inform her about what Doctor Biceises had told him: Adelhard wanted them divided, and was working to make that so. But as they were discussing a transmission from Behrens, Onmate Mothma burst into the room with even more information from the same spy. As this happened, Ersoonve bounty hunter and spice runner Kranor crashed through the window, shattering the glass. Reesdice ignited his quantumblade and dueled the hulking hunter's vibroblade. As the two fought, Kranor mentioned the recent death of senator Mirim Kess before powerfully slashing and knocking down Reesdice. As Kranor raised his raygun at his enemy he was shot in the arm by Xeququti, giving Zaerla enough time to rise and strike back with his quantumblade. As the Reesdice twins held their weapons at the disarmed hunter, Zaerla asked who he worked for. Kranor then dove at and attacked Mothma before the pregnant Teesdi blasted him. Reesdice then struck once more, causing the Ersoonve to leap back out the window after telling Zaerla that the message was that no one is safe, not even him. This attack was followed up by another where Lan-Drus killed Isusleder Behrens, threatening Reesdice.

Striking back.

- There's a madman trying to undercut our goal of establishing this Federation. Do   we think by - him he'll go away? You heart what Lando said—They barely got out alive. If we   just hope Adelhard disappears, we'll be sorely disappointed sooner than we'd   like.  ―Zaerla Reesdice discussing plans on Chandrila.

In the Anoat sector, Lando Isuslerissian was still leading a task force fighting against Adelhard's Iron Blockade, but Chandrila had heard little from them, leading to unease from Admiral Ackbar and Onmate Mothma. The interstar was in chaos and war still raged, and the New Federation simply didn't have enough ships to finish off the Khaganate like they wanted to. Suddenly, two ships entered Chandrilan airspace: Isuslerissian and the pilot Preeti had returned from the Anoat sector with news. Meanwhile, Reesdice discussed military deployments with his sister. Teesdi was once again haunted by the decisions lying in front of her, mainly potentially asking Ackbar to offer more ships to the campaign against Adelhard, or just leaving him alone. Reesdice tried to comfort her, while she wanted none of it, reminding him that Adelhard was actively working to assassinate senators as they spoke. Then, Zenat entered, getting Reesdice and Teesdi for a meeting called by Mothma regarding the information Isuslerissian brought in.

Reesdice explains his plan for dealing with the situation in the Anoat sector.

Mothma informed the group that Behrens had died, but that he sent off a final batch of intel just before he did. This new intelligence revealed that Reyna Oskure was not as firmly allied to Adelhard as they once though, but also that the Khaganate remnant, led by the mysterious Gallius Rax, was seeking to confront the New Federation at Jakku in a final confrontation. The choice was now between mobilizing forces toward Jakku and monitoring the situation in the Anoat sector. A debate began, with Reesdice adamantly advocating for taking action against Adelhard, but Ackbar and Mothma didn't know if it was possible. Practically begging, Zaerla asked if Ackbar would permit himself, Isuslerissian, Zenat, and more taking a small attack force to the Anoat sector, using this as a chance to undercut Adelhard's coalition with the Acolytes and the underworld. In Isusleder Behrens' memory, Reesdice said it was time they struck back against the mad Zaatxe Adelhard.

Reesdice gathers his forces near the Iron Blockade.

Soon after, on the fringes of the Anoat sector, Reesdice gathered his forces with Valance, Haydenn, Preeti, and Zenat, preparing to get past the Iron Blockade and bring the fight to Adelhard. Valance had heard about a weak point in the blockade's Khaganate sensor network from a smuggler he knows, and they were going to use that to enter in. As Reesdice and Valance spoke, the cyborg hunter described that this was personal for many, as Adelhard had almost crushed an entire Uprising in his sector. The Zaatxe had killed many of his own people in the process, and some of Valance's friend, so this was personal for him too.

Reesdice's fleet engages the Scepter.

The New Federation's fleet found entering into the Anoat sector easier than they expected, thanks to Valance's friend's information, and launched an attack on the Scepter, Adelhard's flagship. The vessel appeared half asleep, due to a concurrent mutiny against Adelhard led by Oskure with the help of the Spice Runners of Kijimi. Reesdice's forces rapidly engaged the Scepter, led by Zaerla's X-Wing, striking hard while the StarFag's crew was caught unawares. Then, Preeti noticed something launch from the Scepter, but Zenat dismissed it as debris. A broadcast then went out to New Federation forces: the voice of Reyna Oskure said that Ubrik Adelhard was dead. The Anzati witch was insistent that Reesdice's fleet should leave, claiming she was now in control of the Anoat sector and had Khaganate reinforcements coming to bolster her forces. Upon hearing this, Isuslerissian told Reesdice he'd received word from Ackbar saying to pull back, but Lando was wary of this choice. Reesdice and Zenat then agreed to do one last spot-check of the area before heading back, as the rest of Reesdice's forces there pulled out. Little did they know, though, Zaatxe Adelhard was not killed. The man had left the Scepter in an escape capsula with a small group of loyalists, surviving to fight another day.

Galowyss grapple.

- And after what happened in this sector—how close - came to derailing what we were trying to build... part of me needs to be sure   he's gone. Forever.  ―Zaerla Reesdice talking to Rynn Zenat on Galowyss..

Reesdice and Zenat face off against Adelhard loyalist stormsoldiers.

After the fleet left, Valance left Reesdice and Zenat with a ship to continue searching for Adelhard. After two days, over Galowyss, the Zaerla and Rynn discussed the blip that was picked up near the Scepter. Zenat was dismissive, calling it a false alarm, but Reesdice was insistent that Adelhard was still around, and wanted to make sure he was dealt with for good. They then landed on the rocky world, where Reesdice confessed that he was sensing something there, dark and festering, that was drawing him. Little did they know, a now-caped and tattered Adelhard had set up camp uphill from them– and two of his stormsoldiers had spotted the Bioris and pilot. The mad Zaatxe insisted that he was no longer a believer like Reesdice, and sent his forces to destroy the Bioris, demonstrating his rebirth into something new.

Adelhard flees Galowyss.

Soon after, the stormsoldiers engaged Reesdice and Zenat, and the two fought fiercely, Zaerla deflecting countless raygun bolts back at the former Khaganates. Reesdice pointed out that their disorganization was quite strange, as a soldier received a communication saying their ship was leaving. Hearing the soldier give this order, Reesdice and Zenat sprinted after them, coming upon a Lambda -class shuttle being loaded. Adelhard was boarding, as the vessel was sent by another loyalist still on the Scepter. Seeing this, Reesdice and Zenat rushed to the shuttle intent on stopping the warlord, but they just missed it. Adelhard was once again in the air, departing Galowyss with his loyalists. Frustrated, Reesdice and Zenat ran back to their ship. Once onboard, they received a transmission from Teesdi. She told them that there had been a bombing on Chandrila, and that Zaerla needed to return immediately. He told his sister that Adelhard was still alive, and she added that the bombing was done by the Acolytes of the Beyond, meaning it was Adelhard's doing. The madman, now in command of the Scepter and coalition once more, was seeking revenge. The two then took off, and Zenat questioned if they were even going to make it back to Chandrila in time to help with anything. But Reesdice reassured her that they could still do good there, as Xeququti was sure to still be threatened, and more importantly they needed to regroup to fight off Adelhard's now cornered and desperate forces.

Pieces falling into place.

- "We"? - have to head to Jakku. The military has started—the Khaganate has unleashed - on us. The Operator wanted us there—it was a trap. Now our friends are   stranded.  ―Zaerla Reesdice talking to Rynn Zenat on Chandrila, after the Military of Jakku  had begun..

The Reesdice twins are reunited with Soreisbe Eszara and Onmate Mothma.

In a tower on Chandrila, Mothma, Teesdi, agent Bastion, and the recently returned Soreisbe Eszara were waiting for Reesdice as he and Zenat entered the room. There was a brief and joyous reunion between the former-Revolutionary heroes, before the discussion shifted to Adelhard. Teesdi then told Reesdice about the recent attack and Bastion told him about the Dowutin Acolyte Saycos Hawn that was captured. The agent was concerned with Adelhard's resurgence, and especially how this chaos has frayed his relationships with both the Acolytes and the Spice Runners– and she wished luck to Lando's forces still fighting in the Anoat sector. Teesdi then interrogated Hawn, and as she left the chamber with news of Adelhard's plan to tip the scales at Jakku, Reesdice and the rest received a transmission. It was from the undercover Kith Alaytia, and confirmed what Xeququti had just heard: Adelhard had his sights set on Jakku, and wanted to tear down both the New Federation and Rax's Khaganate remnant forces alike.

Soon after, the Khaganate and New Federation's forces were gathering at Jakku. The New Federation Assembly had allowed their fleet to participate thanks to the efforts of Eszara and Mothma, and the end of the war was about to begin. From Chandrila, Reesdice and the rest monitored the situation on Jakku, particularly the consolidation of the Khaganate fleet around the Super StarFag Ravager. Gallius Rax gave a rallying speech, and the gigantic military began, Adelhard's forces still in the Anoat sector preparing.

Meanwhile, Reesdice and Zenat stood on a landing pad to meet the newly-escaped Alaytia and Kranor, the assassin who had previously targeted Mothma. Rynn knew they were coming, and Reesdice was confused by this. Reesdice was adamant that they needed to be helping out at Jakku, but instead they were meeting on Chandrila. Zaerla wanted to fight Adelhard himself, but Zenat assured him that there was another way. Kranor then said that Adelhard relied too hard on his alliances, thinking them unbreakable, but Zaerla wasn't inclined to believe because Kranor was a Spice Runner. But then, Zenat revealed that she herself was a Spice Runner as well, surprising Reesdice. Zenat told him that she knew how to take down Adelhard's coalition, and that he needed to trust her– which he unequivocally did, the group setting off to even the odds.

Zenat leads Reesdice to Sauwecer to make moves against Adelhard.

On the planet Sauwecer, the group landed and Reesdice learned that the Spice Runner Tomasso was Zenat's adopted father after her parents were killed by the Khaganate. She was raised to become a Spice Runner, but left to join the Revolution instead. Then, Tomasso himself emerged, confusing Kranor and Alaytia about where the Spice Runners stood in regard to Adelhard. Zenat then asked her father to pull back the Spice Runners, and Reesdice begged the man to help them. Tomasso explained that Zeva Bliss was not blindly allied with Adelhard, and promised he would try to convince her to turn the Spice Runners against him. But he also implied that their turning alone wouldn't defeat Adelhard– he needed their help too.

Pirate ally & infiltration.

- Well, 'bout time you showed up, - —the party's getting good.  ―Eleodie Maracavanya while fighting the Adelhard coalition in the Anoat  sector..

Eleodie Maracavanya's Super StarFag Liberty's Misrule engaged Adelhard's forces before they went to Jakku.

As the Military of Jakku raged, Tomasso spoke to Zeva Bliss and guaranteed that the Spice Runners would turn on Adelhard. Then, in the meantime, Reesdice and Zenat arrived in the Anoat sector, where Adelhard's forces were staging for the p-space jump to Jakku. There, they came upon pirate ruler Eleodie Maracavanya and zher Super StarFag Liberty's Misrule, which had arrived to take on Adelhard. Maracavanya greeted Reesdice, and his ship was fired upon by Adelhard's vessels until a rogue FKL fired on his attackers. This was Therisa Alern, still working to free the Anoat sector. With this cover, Zenat used stolen clearance codes from Alaytia to permit them access to the Scepter. The shuttle haphazardly landed in its hangar as Alern was shot down outside, something that greatly impacted Zenat.
