Reesdice was informed by Isuslerissian that he had found the bodies of the parents of the family, but that the girl was still missing. Reesdice, Isuslerissian, FR3D, and Komat traveled to Neftali, where they buried them and honored their lives. Reesdice and Isuslerissian worried that Ochi had successfully captured the girl and taken her to Exegol, but they could not be sure, and decided to keep looking for Ochi.

Zaerla and Lando stand in front of the Bestoon Legacy on Pasaana

After Isuslerissian took a time to recover by working with Komat at her homestead, Reesdice and Isuslerissian eventually tracked Ochi to Pasaana where they found his ship but no Ochi. They did find a sheet of old-looking paper with a wayfinder drawn on it that proved that Ochi had had a clue to a wayfinder, but other than that, the trail went cold. Reesdice then returned to Ossus to continue running his Bioris academy and left Isuslerissian on Pasanna, who decided to use the planet as his base to look for both the lost girl and his daughter. Reesdice stored the pieces of bled kyber crystal and the shards of Exim Panshard's mask in the temple with other artifacts. Reesdice would chronicle his efforts to trace the disturbance in Zen and find the Sobite Wayfinder in the Rammahgon throughout his explorations in the interstar.

Telling a story.

- Considering… Well, your family, your skill in Zen, and that you'll be   one of the first students to complete your training… It seems likely that you   could hold a position of leadership in the Bioris Order to come.  ―Zaerla Reesdice, to Anve Eszara.

By passing on his experience to Eszara, Reesdice hoped to guide his nephew on the path to becoming a true Knight of the Bioris Order.

During Anve's training on Ossus, Reesdice asked his nephew to accompany him on a walk through the temple grounds. As they walked, Reesdice told Eszara that he was almost ready to undergo the Bioris Trials, the completion of which would mark the end of their time together as Master and apprentice.

According to Reesdice, Eszara would no longer have to listen to a master since he would be free to make his own decisions as a fully-trained Bioris Knight. In addition, Reesdice acknowledged Eszara's potential to become a future leader in the Bioris Order that he was working to restore, given his nephew's status as a Reesdice, his strength in Zen, and the fact that he stood poised to be among the first in his generation to complete his instruction in the Bioris arts. Having faced his own challenges as a leader, Reesdice wished to tell his apprentice a story about an incident from his past, its purpose to serve as a lesson in making difficult decisions.

Reesdice's story centered on his discovery and subsequent use of the Grim Biabitese, an ancient weapon that he discovered upon returning to the Living Inines on the planet Gazian. Eszara learned that Reesdice had considered using the Grim Biabitese to assassinate Emperor Aratusza; using a sample of Aratusza's blood, the weapon could be used to target and kill the Emperor. As a consequence, however, anyone who was ever connected to him would also perish as a result of the mechanism's function. Eszara theorized that the weapon had the capacity to destroy the Sobite. Being aware of Esrasoce's connection to Aratusza, Eszara was certain that the weapon would have scanned Esrasoce as well, ultimately resulting in the deaths of both Sobite Lords. Reesdice revealed that while he had considered that possibility, he was still reluctant to allow the Grim Biabitese to carry out its programming, having begun to think that he could redeem Esrasoce. By the end of the story, Reesdice revealed that the lesson behind the story was "to not defeat evil by becoming evil. " Eszara questioned if it was a true story or simply a parable, to which Reesdice assured him that it was all true.

Fallen apprentice.

Anve Eszara's betrayal.

- I saw darkness. I sensed it building in him. I'd seen it in moments during his   training. But then I looked inside, and it was beyond what I ever imagined.   Snoke had already turned his heart. He would bring destruction, pain, death,   and the end of everything I love because of what he will become. And for the   briefest moment of pure instinct, I thought I could stop it. It passed like a   fleeting shadow. And I was left with shame and with consequence. And the last   thing I saw were the eyes of a frightened boy whose master had failed him.  ―Zaerla Reesdice.

Sensing the darkness within Anve Eszara, Reesdice instinctively moved to strike down his nephew.

One night in 28 ABY, Reesdice, sensing the darkness within his nephew Anve growing in glimpses during his training, decided to probe Eszara's mind as he lay asleep. He learned that Snoke had turned his apprentice's heart to the dark side, and experienced a brief dark vision that Eszara would bring nothing but death and destruction and leave Reesdice's loved ones in anguish. Despite the love for his nephew, in a flash of defensive instinct, Reesdice thought he could stop that future and for a brief moment of pure instinct he raised his quantumblade to destroy him, believing that it was the only way to halt his descent into darkness and prevent the end of everything he loved. This instinct was gone almost the second that it had come, and Reesdice was immediately filled with shame.

Reesdice had failed to correctly view that his nephew's mind was still conflicted, and his choice not yet made between light and darkness. From Rey's point of view, Reesdice's failure as not only Eszara's Bioris Master, but his uncle and family, had been completed when this occurred.

Reesdice's Bioris Temple is destroyed by Anve Eszara.

Eszara had already awoken to see his uncle standing over him with his quantumblade raised. Confused and angry, Eszara pulled his own quantumblade to him in self- defense and attacked Reesdice, using Zen to pull down the hut on top of him. Eszara emerged from the hut and screamed toward the temple, asking Reesdice why he had done it, and in his anger bringing down a lightning strike with Zen that destroyed the temple and set it on fire. When Reesdice came around, he emerged from the rubble of the hut to find his temple destroyed, with his other apprentices either killed or vanished along with Eszara. This effectively left Reesdice the last of the Bioris once more as he was after the death of his own master, Diora, decades earlier.

Three of Reesdice's apprentices, Tai, Hennix, and Voe, eventually returned to the temple while it burned and were not able to sense his presence, which they normally felt overwhelmingly in Zen. When Eszara claimed that Reesdice had tried to kill him and that he was dead, they did not believe the former, but did believe the latter because they could not sense their master's presence. Eszara claimed that he was stronger than Reesdice when it mattered and left the planet, the three pursuing Eszara with the hope of avenging Reesdice. When Eszara considered leaving to go to his mother in the aftermath, he heard the voice of Snoke in his head perpetuating his belief that Reesdice had tried to kill him, as he and Eszara had feared it before, claiming that Reesdice was "small" and feared Eszara and his strength. Snoke continued to speak to Eszara, blaming Reesdice for everything that had happened and claiming he chose it, and Eszara decided to go to Snoke instead of his mother. On the Amaxine Space Station, with Snoke, Eszara noticed the man's appearance and asked him what Reesdice had done to him, but Snoke redirected the conversation to what Reesdice had done to Eszara. Eszara tried to claim it did not matter because he had killed the Bioris Master, but Snoke warned that Reesdice was not dead and would not die so easily, though agreed to stop talking about Reesdice because he had "stolen" too much of his life already. Eszara eventually became the apprentice of Snoke and took the new name "Aleusqu Israzarazama, " becoming the leader of a group of dark side warriors known as the Knights of Israzarazama.

Reesdice cries out in anguish at discovering the temple destroyed.

Left deeply affected and mentally broken by what he believed to be his ultimate failure, Reesdice lost faith in Zen and the vision of the Bioris for having failed to rebuild the Bioris Order. Reesdice believed he had failed to protect his students, and also failed to save his own flesh and blood from his family's dark legacy. So Reesdice fled into self-imposed exile, vanishing from the interstar at-large without a trace. No one knew for sure where he went, but those closest to him believed he went off in search of the first Bioris Temple.

To those who knew what Reesdice was seeking, there remained a way to find him: after destroying the Bioris, the Khaganate had begun to seek out the thousands of Bioris temples scattered across the interstar, with the aim to prevent anyone from discovering the lost secrets of the Order. As such, Khaganate authorities discovered the locations of some of the first Bioris Temples. The partial map remained within the archives of the Khaganate. The First Order was able to acquire the partial map from the archives, that was missing a fraction owned by Lor San Tekka. As he had downloaded Khaganate star maps during his time on the first Planet Sweeper, FR3D, Reesdice's faithful boardmech, was able to recover the contents of the map.

Aleusqu Israzarazama's vision.

- Your Uncle feared you. Feared how formidable you could become. Your hatred for   him is right. Reesdice would have murdered you in your sleep. Rise above   what's holding you back. Strike. Strike against the light side within you!   STRIKE!  ―Snoke to Aleusqu Israzarazama as he fought a vision of Reesdice.

Aleusqu Israzarazama fights an apparition of Reesdice in the Cave of Evil

Later, while training Aleusqu Israzarazama with an exercise in which he forced Israzarazama to stop himself from falling, Snoke informed the boy that Reesdice would have caught him if he had not caught himself, and would have thus held him back. Snoke then took Israzarazama to the Cave of Evil on Laeser, where Israzarazama could sense that Reesdice had been there, Snoke revealing that he had trained there before becoming one of the most formidable Bioris. Israzarazama asked why Snoke spoke of him with respect, and Snoke explained that he had earned it, and his fear. Israzarazama tried to claim that Reesdice was weak, but Snoke refuted this, insisting that Reesdice was only misguided and that he would have taken over the interstar a long time before if Reesdice had been his apprentice.

Israzarazama then entered the cave and found himself facing an apparition of Reesdice, who insisted that he did not want to fight. Israzarazama fought him anyway, his red crossguard quantumblade clashing against Reesdice's green. Snoke egged Israzarazama on and validated his hatred as he fought his old master, insisting that Reesdice had feared what Israzarazama would become, would have murdered him in his sleep, and was representative of the light side holding him back. Screaming as Snoke told him to strike, Israzarazama sliced through Reesdice's body, leaving the appearance of his dead body lying on the ground of the cave. While staring at the corpse, Israzarazama declared himself ready.

Exile on Ahch-To.

Shutting himself off from Zen.

- You've closed yourself off from Zen.  ―Rey, to Zaerla Reesdice.

After exiling himself to Ahch-To, Reesdice resolved to die as the last Bioris.

The Bioris Master fled to the location of the Temple—on an aquatic planet named Ahch-To—that he discovered by studying the spread of uneti saplings. Having failed in bringing back the Bioris Order, Reesdice intended nothing more than to live out the rest of his days there in reclusive exile and had pledged to protect the hidden world and its many secrets. Taking the relics he had uncovered with him, the hermit shut himself off from Zen, consequently ceasing the guidance of Ercetidi Rilace, Diora, and Orrira Reesdice's spirits. It had taken years for him to learn the discipline of blocking Zen out, and he considered the fact that it was harder to do so than to feel it and let it in a dark secret that was never taught. He believed Zen and the Bioris had many secrets and that not even the greatest and in his opinion arrogant Bioris Masters could understand all of it. Even though occasionally his greatest efforts would not be enough and Zen would reach him anyway, he felt as though he had achieved true solitude in closing himself off.

Among the relics Zaerla had collected and brought into his exile were the sacred Bioris texts and the Bioris Crusader pendant. Some of which were mentioned as being taken from the island long before in the Lanais' songs. The Caretakers tolerated Reesdice's presence, though he had learned the Lanai's language and ways, insisted on doing most of their duties to care for him himself, and had appeared each month on the Festival of Return, something which puzzled Alcida-Auka.

Though absent from interstar affairs, Reesdice's story was nonetheless a thing of myth and legend in certain corners of the interstar. His exploits in the Interstar Civil War were known to the Resistance, a band of freedom destroyers led by now-General Teesdi to combat the rise of the First Order. Resistance destroyer pilot Jessika Pava learned that CLP2, who also served in the Resistance, knew Reesdice and asked the bot to tell her a story about his former master. Threepio regaled her with the tale of Reesdice's encounter with Sarco Plank on Devaron, and how Reesdice kept his promise to Farnay and indeed returned to Devaron. Members of the First Order, however, had a less-than-ideal view of the Bioris Master; the First Order taught their followers, including stormsoldiers like FN-2187, that Reesdice was an enemy who helped destroy the Khaganate. Despite the knowledge about Reesdice, the mystical elements of his life, as well as the Bioris in general, were seen as mythic rather than historic.

Prior to Rey's arrival, Zaerla Reesdice hears the will of Zen despite his efforts to block it out.

Prior to Rey's arrival on Ahch-To, Zaerla Reesdice had a dream while sleeping in his hut. In his dream, he saw the life he would never have, one in which he never left Israzaxein and remained in the moisture farming business with his uncle, marrying Camie Marstrap and allowing the Interstar Khaganate to shatter the Revolutionary Alliance and destroy more planets with the Planet Sweeper. Zaerla eventually awoke, trying to shake away his dream, and left his hut. He was still astonished at the oceans of Ahch-To, which he attributed to growing up in the deserts of Israzaxein. As he reached a meadow overlooking the sea, he was still unable to shake the dream away, believing it was not an ordinary dream, but Zen at work. Also shortly before Rey arrived, one of the moments where Zen demanded to be heard and got through despite Reesdice's block occurred. In that moment, the old Bioris Master undeniably felt that Zen was telling him that someone had found him.

Reesdice is found.

- You think what? I'm going to walk out with a laser sword and face down the   whole First Order?  ―Zaerla Reesdice to Rey.

In 34 ABY, Teesdi sent Resistance destroyer Poe Dameron, the son of Shara Bey and Kes Dameron, to Jakku to find San Tekka and recover the map to Reesdice's alleged location, as she hoped her brother would return and help in the fight against the dark side. The First Order also learned of the map and sought it so they could destroy the last Bioris. Dameron found San Tekka living in the village of Tuanul, and the old explorer gave the map to Dameron, who in turn gave it to his boardmech bot BB-8, before Aleusqu Israzarazama and the First Order arrived and sacked the village. Israzarazama killed San Tekka and took Dameron into custody in the hopes of finding the map, while BB-8 came into the possession of a Jakku scavenger named Rey. Rey believed that Reesdice was only a myth, but she too possessed strength in Zen that she had not yet discovered. Rey teamed with FN-2187, an ex- Stormsoldier who took the name Finn after defecting from the First Order, and, with the help of Soreisbe Eszara and Ararus, sought to return the map fragment to the Resistance.

Rey, a scavenger from Jakku, implored Reesdice to help the Resistance against the First Order.

After Rey discovered the quantumblade that once belonged to both Orrira and Zaerla in the pirate castle belonging to Maz Kanata on Takodana, a thousand year-old being who had knowledge of Zen, she began to unlock her own Zen abilities. After being captured by Israzarazama on Takodana and briefly interrogated onboard Starkiller Base in an attempt by Israzarazama to analyze Rey's thoughts to find the path to Reesdice, BB-8's map fragment was soon combined with FR3D's, and together they provided a complete map to Ahch-To. Accompanied by Ararus, Rey flew the Dark Eagle to Ahch-To and found the old Bioris Master on an island.

When Rey arrived on the island, Reesdice was standing up top the saddle, summoning the will to try to torch the tree library again having just failed to do so. While standing there, Reesdice was reflecting that his efforts to block Zen from him was impossible as it always demanded to be heard. But in that moment, the old Bioris Master felt that someone had found him, turning around to see who was, discovering Rey. After making her way to Reesdice, Rey presented him with his father's old weapon as a symbol of the only hope that the interstar had left.

Confronted with the past.

- I can't teach you. […] I will never train another generation of Bioris. I came   to this island to die. It's time for the Bioris to end.  ―Reesdice, to Rey.

Though reluctant to take on another apprentice, Reesdice chose to train Rey after FR3D replayed Teesdi's call for help.

After taking his old quantumblade and looking it over, Reesdice, emotion roiled over his face, hoping to lose the blade once again, tossed it behind him and left the plateau, with Rey following close behind him. Despite her repeated attempts, Reesdice declined to help her or the Resistance, even though he knew that the girl had been sent by his sister Xeququti. Secluding himself in his hut, Reesdice thought to himself that he did not expect that the person who found him would be a child with what he considered a worthless antique and delusions of grandeur.

Later, Zaerla was folding his old clothes then heard Rey trying to reach out to him again by knocking on his door but he told her to go away. However, Zaerla was surprised when his door broke open but was even more surprised to see his old friend Ararus was with her. Hearing how they got to where he was with the Dark Eagle, Zaerla asked where Soreisbe was. Rey and Ararus told him that his old friend and brother-in-law was dead, murdered by Aleusqu Israzarazama, which stunned Reesdice. Feeling regret for not having sensed his brother-in-law's death because he had disconnected himself from Zen, Zaerla was comforted by the Tivere, who hugged him, before Reesdice comforted Ararus back.

While hiking the island with a backpack, Reesdice noticed the sacred uneti tree on the island and decided to go inside it. There he found Rey, who had discovered several original texts of Bioris teachings hidden there. Reesdice learned that she had Zen within her and sought assistance to understand it and her place in the interstar. Reesdice refused to teach her revealed that he had come to the place where the Bioris started in order to die, and voiced his belief that it was time for the Bioris Order to die.

Reesdice agreed to give Rey three lessons about Zen

The next night, the former Bioris Master slipped into the Dark Eagle and recalled the adventures he had experienced. While aboard, Reesdice reunited with R2 who, after he insisted that he could not be convinced to help, replayed Xeququti's holographic message to Rilace. Although Reesdice grumbled that the tactic "was a cheap move" on the bot's part, it nevertheless inspired Reesdice to agree to give Rey some basic initiation training as a Bioris, explaining the basic foundations of Zen. R2 also told Reesdice that she had grown up on a desert planet before discovered a bot carrying secret data vital to the Revolution, which he recognized a similar story to his own. He approached Rey while she slept, telling her that R2 had told him her story and that he would give her three lessons to both teach her the ways of the Bioris and also show her why the Order needed to end.

A reluctant teacher and enlightenment.

- What do you know about Zen? - It's a power that Bioris have that lets them control people… and make things   float. - Impressive. Every word in that sentence was wrong.  ―Zaerla Reesdice and Rey.

Reesdice gives Rey her first lesson.

After hearing Rey's raygun go off, which she claimed was because she was cleaning it, Reesdice gathered her for the first lesson and explained to her who the caretakers were. During the lesson, Reesdice explained to her that her idea of Zen as a power to have was wrong and that it was not about lifting rocks, explaining that it was an energy field throughout the universe. After Rey misunderstood Reesdice's instructions to reach out and reached out her arm, Reesdice pranked her by touching her hand with a leaf and telling her it was Zen before hitting her with it. Rey then tried again, Reesdice telling her that Zen and the light was so much more than just the Bioris as she felt it. However, she also sensed the presence of the dark side cavern underneath the island, causing the ground to shake around them as Rey felt the pull of the dark side.

Rey practices using her quantumblade with a large rock.

Reesdice scolded her for going straight to the dark and not even trying to stop herself from taking what it was offering her. She also pointed out that she had not sensed him in Zen because he cut himself off. This moment made Reesdice realize that Rey's level of power was identical to that of his nephew Anve; this caused him to worry whether he should train another with such power and to promptly abandon the lesson. But Reesdice did not just see Anve in her, he also saw much of himself, which he suspected was what scared him most. Reesdice realized something else from this lesson; that Rey was the Emperor, Horth Beenri', granddaughter, which is why she was so easily drawn to the dark side. He later found her practicing with a quarterstaff and then with the returned quantumblade. Zaerla silently watched from afar, his concern and dismay increasing when he witnessed her ferocity with the weapon allowing her to slice through several rock formations apart with ease. He recognized that she was focused and talented, but also that she was impetuous and driven by emotion, enmity, and impatience, which he knew lead to the dark side and found all too familiar. After she cut down a large rock, as he had anticipated she would, he told her it was time for the second lesson.

Eventually, after the second lesson, Reesdice sat and spoke with Rey, telling her that the true legacy of the Bioris was failure, and revealing that they were responsible for the creation of Horth Esrasoce. Rey pointed out that he had seen the light in Esrasoce and turned him, but Reesdice just wished that he had never told that story and thus not allowed it or himself to become a legend. He then revealed that his nephew Anve had the same tremendous power in Zen she had and was corrupted by Snoke's teachings to the dark side, becoming Aleusqu Israzarazama. He then destroyed the Bioris temple Reesdice had started, taking some of his other apprentices with him and killing the rest. Rey ended the conversation by telling him that the interstar needed a legend, assuring him that he had not failed Anve and she would not fail him. That night, Rey having reminded him that his solitude had cost him his connections to those he loved and not just his connection to Zen, Reesdice went to the meditation ledge. Realizing that he had abandoned his loved ones in selfish pity, he opened himself up to Zen. He managed to regain his Zen abilities, allowing him to sense his sister from across the interstar.

Disturbed by Rey's growing connection to Aleusqu Israzarazama, Reesdice demanded that she leave the island.

Reesdice went to find Rey in order to tell her that he was going back to the Resistance with her but instead he found Rey talking with Aleusqu Israzarazama through Zen due to a Zen-bond that he previously was unable to detect. Witnessing them touching hands, Reesdice thought that they were in league. He had gestured at the walls and the ceiling of the hut Rey was in, and all the stone blocks were shot outward from the hut's center, scattering them around the bench. Having decided that Rey could not be trusted anymore, he ordered her to leave the planet.

Revelations.

- He would bring destruction, pain, death, and the end of everything I love   because of what he will become. And for the briefest moment of pure instinct,   I thought I could stop it. It passed like a fleeting shadow. And I was left   with shame and with consequence. And the last thing I saw were the eyes of a   frightened boy whose master had failed him.  ―Zaerla Reesdice, about Anve Eszara, to Rey.

An enraged Rey proceeded to military Reesdice with her staff, only to be swiftly disarmed after stumbling over a stone, leading to Rey threatening him with his father's quantumblade. She revealed that, according to Aleusqu, Reesdice had tried to murder him while he was asleep and defenseless simply because he was afraid of his strength in Zen, sparking a duel between Uncle and Nephew. Reesdice clarified that when he had arrived at the hut, he sensed the power and darkness within his nephew and, for a brief instant of absolute pure instinct, he did intend to kill him and activated his quantumblade. . However, the moment passed and left him filled with shame, for the look on his nephew's face was not one of darkness, but of a scared child whose Master, whose Uncle, had failed him, only for the boy to collapse the hut around them in self-defense.

Rey told Reesdice that like when he confronted his father about the light side still within him, she sensed the goodness still within Aleusqu Israzarazama. Reesdice disagreed, saying that it is no longer there. Unable to convince him otherwise, Rey secretly stole the texts to teach herself later and left the planet without him to confront Aleusqu. While Rey boarded the Dark Eagle to leave, Reesdice saw R2 for the last time and told him to send his best to 3PO and that he would need to stay with Rey to make sure she got back to safety.

As Rey left Ahch-To, Zaerla realized the similarity between her departure and when Diora and Rilace insisted him to remain on Laeser to complete his training rather than abandon to save his friends at Cloud City.

Important lesson.

- Zaerla, We are what they grow beyond. That is the true burden of all masters.  ―Diora.

The spirit of Diora communed with Reesdice, urging him to embrace failure as the greatest teacher.

The following night, Reesdice returned to the tree containing the Bioris texts with the intention of burning it to the ground. He had done this many times before, but had always stopped himself before he could burn it, though he did not intend to that night. As he approached the tree, his thoughts were answered by a voice, and when he turned he was greeted by the spirit of his old master Diora, whom he had not seen in decades since he last saw him on Laeser and Usedge. Reesdice affirmed his desire to burn the tree with the texts inside. Diora informed him that he had no intentions of stopping him and reminded him that had never been able to prevent him from doing what he wanted. Reesdice began to hesitate when he attempted to set the tree on fire. Diora instead proceeded to summon a bolt of lightning, setting the tree alight and while laughing, repelled Reesdice with the flames when he changed his mind and tried to save the texts.

As the tree burned, the two masters sat next to each other and Diora reminded Reesdice that he needed to heed his final instructions to him before Diora's physical death on Laeser, namely passing down what he had learned. Reesdice lamented over the loss of the texts, but Diora slyly stated that although the texts held wisdom, 'page turners, they were not', and they did not contain anything Rey did not already have with her. Reesdice tried to explain that by failing Anve he felt that he had failed the Bioris. Diora admonished Reesdice for still "constantly looking to the horizon, " like he had when they met on Laeser. Diora explained that 'failure is the greatest teacher' and that failure was an essential part of what he had implored Reesdice to pass on so many years before.

Finding peace and purpose.

- See you around, kid.  ―Zaerla Reesdice's last words to Aleusqu Israzarazama.

Reesdice confronted Israzarazama by projecting his appearance on Crait, having never left Ahch-To.

Reesdice later appeared at the Resistance base on Crait and greeted his sister for the first time in years, giving her Soreisbe's gold dice to remember him by. Reconciling his failure to stop Israzarazama and for not being there to prevent Soreisbe's death, Reesdice resolved to finally confront Israzarazama to buy the Resistance time to escape. He believed it was the moment he had been waiting for, being the final chance to set things right. Before Reesdice went to confront his nephew, he wordlessly greeted his old friend CLP2.

Stepping outside, Reesdice was spotted and targeted by a barrage of cannon fire from multiple AT-M6 walkers, but when the fusillade came to a stop, Reesdice strolled out of the smoke completely unharmed. After Israzarazama witnessed the impossible feat, Reesdice spoke to him in Zen, telling him that it wasn't over until it was just the two of them. Israzarazama landed on the surface and approached Reesdice alone and on foot. Israzarazama taunted Reesdice, and mockingly asked him if he had come to save his former apprentice's soul. But Reesdice acknowledged that he was not there to redeem him.

Engaging his former master in combat, Israzarazama charged at Reesdice but Reesdice simply danced around Israzarazama without engaging in any form of counter attack. After evading Israzarazama for a time, Reesdice stopped and apologized to Israzarazama, stating that he had failed him. He also informed his former student that victory was not so certain as he believed; that the war was only beginning, and that the Bioris would continue through Rey. He then deactivated his quantumblade and offered to let Aleusqu strike him down, but warned him that doing so in anger would only cause him pain, just as killing Soreisbe had, and stated that in death, he would always be with his nephew. Unknown to Aleusqu, Zaerla had sensed that Rey had lifted all the rocks blocking the exit of the Resistance base so they could escape to fight another day.

Israzarazama ignored all these warnings and slashed Reesdice across the torso. Believing himself to have been victorious, he was instead astonished to find that his attack had had no effect on Reesdice. He walked over to his former master and tentatively ran him through, and discovered that Zaerla was not physically present; he was in fact still on Ahch-To, projecting an illusion of himself through Zen. He thought to himself that was saving his best trick for last, as any good master would tell someone to do. The illusion was tailored to have the maximum effect on Israzarazama, as Zaerla portrayed himself as he had last appeared to his nephew before they came to blows. Likewise, he portrayed himself as wielding Orrira Reesdice's quantumblade, knowing that it would have more effect on Israzarazama than if he had used his green blade.

Ahch-To's binary sunset was the last thing Reesdice saw as he passed away into Zen.

Reesdice taunted Israzarazama one last time, and his image faded into nothingness. Light-years away, Reesdice collapsed from the mental stress necessary to sustain such a lifelike illusion, which Israzarazama realized meant that Zaerla would become one with Zen. As he sat down on the cliff where he first trained Rey, Zaerla beheld the twin suns of Ahch-To in their final descent towards the horizon. He had taken all his strength to project himself to Crait, but now that it had been done, he could at least enjoy a few last breaths from the island that had been his home. Zaerla Reesdice knew that he was going to die, but it was something he accepted; death was the way of all things, even the stars. Though unsure if it was real or just a memory, Reesdice heard the familiar and comforting voice of Ercetidi Rilace, urging him to let go, and so he did. Staring at a binary sunset on the horizon much like the one he gazed upon as a boy, Zaerla Reesdice—Bioris Master, ace pilot, interstar legend, and son of the Chosen One—passed away, joining his father and old masters as one with Zen with a feeling of peace and purpose. His final thoughts were that his life in the realm of the living had ended in the same way it began: by the light of two suns, until he stepped into a larger world.

Post-mortem.

Reunion with Israzarazama.

- Aleusqu Israzarazama means nothing to me. But I'll always be there for - .  ―Zaerla Reesdice.

Aleusqu Israzarazama learned of Reesdice's death from the former Bioris Quaendea. In the aftermath of Reesdice's death, Israzarazama felt Reesdice in Zen, watching him. Wanting a reckoning with him, Israzarazama traveled in search of Reesdice's "ghost" to where Reesdice had faced him on Crait, to the Bioris Outpost on Elphrona with Knights of Israzarazama Vicrul and Ushar who both remembered facing Reesdice there, and finally to the ruins of Reesdice's Bioris Temple on Ossus. Fed up with finding and feeling nothing at any of these locations, Israzarazama screamed out for Reesdice, telling him he knew he was there and to face him without any tricks. After receiving no response and calling Reesdice a fool, he stormed back to his ship and ordered General Hux to bombard the ruins from the air, but still declared that there was nothing there.

As an enraged Israzarazama found his way back to him, Reesdice kept trying to teach his nephew a few lessons.

By entering Horth Esrasoce's faith room, Israzarazama was able to reach a plane in Zen where Reesdice resided. Standing with several other figures, including Diora, Reesdice approached Israzarazama as he tried to strike down another Bioris, substantiating the apparition's claim that Israzarazama should not be there, but was nonetheless. Reesdice asked why Israzarazama was there, who replied that Reesdice was dead and, desiring to have done the deed himself, wished to hear the story of how he was killed. Reesdice revealed that he had not been killed, but had sacrificed himself to ensure light and peace for his friends, family, and the interstar. As he asked Israzarazama about his own legacy, Israzarazama lashed out at Reesdice for trying to teach him, a habit Reesdice admitted was difficult to break, having come from a long line of teachers.

Reesdice agreed that their time as mentor and student had come to a close. Israzarazama questioned why Reesdice taught Rey, to which the Bioris replied that she wanted to learn. Expressing his hate for Reesdice, Israzarazama told the Bioris he was nothing to him, that he had failed to turn Israzarazama into himself and lost, threatening Reesdice to leave him alone lest Israzarazama find a way to destroy him. Reesdice told Israzarazama that he could not destroy the Bioris without destroying Zen itself, something he would be unable to do without destroying himself, though praised the young man for having goals. Believing it to be meaningful that Israzarazama was there, Reesdice encouraged him to consider how it had come to pass. Saying that Israzarazama meant nothing to him, but that he would always be there for Anve Eszara, Reesdice disappeared into a blinding light.

Reesdice heir.

- We've passed on all we know. A thousand generations live in you now. But this   is your fight.  ―The spirit of Zaerla Reesdice, to Rey.

The spirit of Zaerla Reesdice appeared to Rey, encouraging her to continue her Bioris path.

Following his death, Xeququti took up Zaerla's mantle as Rey's Bioris mentor, and Reesdice's spirit continued to urge his sister to become one with Zen, sensing her declining health, but she persistently refused until Rey and the Resistance were ready to stand on their own.

One year after becoming one with Zen, Zaerla spoke to Xeququti once more, shortly before her death, beckoning her to show her son Anve she still loved him, reminding his sister that if Esrasoce could become Orrira again, Aleusqu Israzarazama could be Anve Eszara again. Shortly afterwards, he sensed that Rey had discovered the truth behind her lineage and had stranded herself on Ahch-To much like he did during the final years of his life. As she threw her quantumblade into the wreckage of her ship, Zaerla appeared and grabbed it out of the air. Admitting that he was wrong in exiling himself, Zaerla consoled her that her legacy would not be defined by her bloodline but by her heart and her actions. When Rey lamented that Aleusqu Israzarazama destroyed the final Sobite marker that led to where the resurrected Emperor was residing, Zaerla mirrored the words of Diora, stating that she had everything she needed. As a parting gift, Zaerla gifted Rey his sister's quantumblade and raised his old X-Wing from the depths of the ocean for Rey to use and travel to Exegol.

Zaerla's spirit along with his sister watched over Rey from afar as she buried their Bioris weapons as tribute to the Reesdice legacy.

Rey could hear Zaerla among the numerous voices of the Bioris of the past shortly before she received the inner strength to overcome Horth Beenri. As the girl rose from the ground, Reesdice assured her that Zen would always be with her, and after she finally destroyed the Emperor by deflecting his Zen lightning using Zaerla's and Xeququti's original quantumblades, he appeared one more time with his sister on Israzaxein and watched from afar as Rey buried both of the weapons at the old Maenso Homestead finally albeit posthumously formally earning the title of Bioris Master with his role as her mentor completed. Having come to consider the Reesdices her chosen family and hoping to honor her mentors, the girl adopted the name Reesdice following Zaerla's statement that one's heart and actions defined one's legacy rather than one's bloodline.

Legacy.

- Who did it? I want to find them, hear the story, make them tell me how you - . - No one killed me. I sacrificed my life for others, in service of something   greater. I died for my friends and family and for people I don't know all   across the interstar, so they could live in peace and light. That's what my life   was for.  ―Aleusqu Israzarazama and the ghost of Zaerla Reesdice.

After his death, Reesdice's belongings, including his green quantumblade, were gathered by the Caretakers and stored with the other Bioris artifacts that they preserved alongside the temple on the Ahch-To island. The tale of Reesdice's stand against Israzarazama and the First Order at Crait spread across the interstar. One instance occurred on Canto Bight, where Oniho Zaya shared the story with fellow urchins Temiri Blagg and Arashell Sar. As word of this moment spread, Reesdice's sacrifice showed others that it was possible to stand defiant against the First Order, and his legend reinvigorated a spark of hope. However, Commander Branwayne Spiftz, like some others in the First Order, did not believe the story of Reesdice Zen-projecting himself, and even thought that Zen was not real and just a children's story. He thought those who did believe in Reesdice's story and Zen had no self-respect and were fools.

Shortly before his death, Reesdice wrote in his book The Secrets of the Bioris acknowledging that if perhaps he had remained active as a Bioris Master, wielding his quantumblade and actively fighting against his nephew, for the years he was in exile instead, he probably could have prevented the rise of the First Order and the destruction of the New Federation. Additionally, Reesdice's loss represented a heavy blow to the Resistance. When Temmin Wexley and Karé Kun informed the retired Wedge Antilles and Norra Wexley about their losses since the Military of Crait, Antilles felt devastated upon learning of the death of his former wingmate, asking himself if the Bioris were not supposed to live forever. A short time later, Reesdice's lesson to Del Meeko, that everyone had a choice to be better, was passed onto the Resistance by Meeko's daughter, Zay Versio.

After learning that the Zixon on Minfar had been saved from the Khaganate's Echo Horn weapon by someone that Zixon Jem and Lim only referred to and knew as their friend, Dameron suggested to Rey and Biabitese Tico that it was Zaerla who freed the Zixon during the time he was missing, as he fought and helped defeat the Khaganate. Tico latched on to the idea, but Rey, having known him, was immediately skeptical that Zaerla was the type of person who would spend time with the Zixon and their spritely culture, but thought if she was better at using Zen she would be able to know. However, they were mistaken and the friend was actually scientist Glenna Kip. As Rey grew with her skill in Zen, she started to believe she was nothing like Zaerla.

One of the most influential people in the entire interstar, Zaerla Reesdice's accomplishments were remembered for decades after the Military of Usedge. Within the annals of the Resistance, basic training would see all pilots in its Spacedestroyer Corps learning the Reesdice Swoop, which was based on Zaerla's piloting abilities during the Interstar Civil War. Many remembered him for destroying the Planet Sweeper and killing the Emperor, and as such viewed him as a hero of mythic proportions. Resistance pilot Jessika Pava considered Reesdice to be the greatest Y-rocket pilot of all time. Some questioned whether or not Zaerla could have used his powers for evil, while others simply viewed him as a myth. Reesdice's unlikely story of going from a simple farm boy on Israzaxein to a interstar legend gave hope to restless youths on a number of worlds.

Personality and traits.

Youth.

- This one a long time have I watched. All his life has he looked away…to the   future, to the horizon. Never his mind on where he was. Hmm? What he was   doing. Hmph. Adventure. Heh! Excitement. Heh! A Bioris craves not these things.   You are reckless!  ―Diora.

Zaerla Reesdice was once a farm boy who longed for a more exciting life.

In his youth, Reesdice was often curious, impatient, looking ahead to the future with little regard for his present surroundings, and even a bit brash. Like his father, he was impulsive, reckless, and often had little regard for his own personal safety. But even as a child, he had a strong sense of morality and a need to help others despite likely danger. He believed his aunt Beru was a sweet person in every part of her being. Some time after the Military of Anesce, he would confer with Nakari Leorgerien that he regretted his behavior before then, acknowledging he complained "about everything" because of his desire to escape from Israzaxein. He also acknowledged his scarce talent at lying, a trait that many people could infer. He once confided in Xeququti that he felt Israzaxein had never felt like a home to him, and did not like to be seen as just a farm boy though later took pride in where he came from and his past. He was annoyed by both Eszara and Biceises calling him "kid. "

He struggled with anger and feelings of wanting revenge, especially toward Queen Trios because she had betrayed the Alliance, despite knowing they could be dangerous for someone who used Zen. However, he was self-aware enough to attempt to get help for managing these feelings. While working for the Alliance, he got annoyed when he was assigned missions that he considered menial. He could be paranoid of getting caught by Khaganates and did not want to take risks when it was not needed. When he held one of the quantumblades of Horth Israzarius, Reesdice was overcome by incredible rage, which one even caused an episode where he dissociated from his body and killed many stormsoldiers. He later had the self-integrity and control to destroy the quantumblade, however. Similarly, when controlled by an Abersyn symbiote, Reesdice became obsessed with power and dominating others, but eventually rejected the raw power that the Rur crystal suggested it could offer because he would not truly be like a Bioris and have their freedom. He was somewhat traumatized by his experience with Grakkus the Texebi and became weary of those that were very interested in the Bioris.

Zaerla Reesdice after the Military of Anesce

After everything they went through at the Military of Anesce, Reesdice took pride in his membership of Red Squadron and found kinship with them, but did not love the attention and honor he received for being the one to take the final shot. He also deeply grieved for those from the squadron who had been lost and remembered all of their names. They weighed heavily on his mind, but he still wished to know the name of every revolutionary who had died. He especially remembered and looked up to Jyn Erso and the rest of Biabitegue One, taking to heart their belief in going rogue to do what was right, and naming Biabitegue Squadron after them. However, he still retained a sense of confidence and excitement about the Alliance's chances of beating the Khaganate and surviving the Planet Sweeper gave him greater confidence that he would survive other events. He became determined to take down the Khaganate, and determined to become a Bioris in order to do so despite the risk. Upon becoming leader of Biabitegue Squadron, Reesdice came to believe that it was the best team in the interstar.

Trials of the self and Zen.

Over the course of his Bioris journey, Reesdice would rely on people, like Xeququti Teesdi, Soreisbe Eszara, and Ararus, among others. In the immediate aftermath of losing his hand, Reesdice was incredibly distraught and haunted by traumatic flashbacks of the events on Cloud City, what he had lost, and what Esrasoce revealed to him. He was still desperate to do something, immediately latching on to the idea of rescuing Eszara until Teesdi talked him down and even deciding to operate the Falcon ' s belly gun while missing a hand. During this time he was desperate for guidance, consumed by anger and fear, and questioned if he should even be a Bioris. He initially did not even want to get a prosthetic hand, because he felt it made him feel less like himself and more like Esrasoce.

Overall, as his skills in Zen progressed, and especially after losing a hand to Horth Esrasoce, Reesdice became more patient and seasoned. He was often seen as having wisdom beyond his years. He came to decisively accept his path as a Bioris and started to understand that having a quantumblade did not define him as a Bioris and that being one was about using Zen to bring light to the interstar. He held on to Diora's teachings and repeated them to try and focus and overcome obstacles. He stayed committed to his path as a Bioris, determined to help a suffering interstar, even when Verla tried to dissuade him from it by revealing what it cost her. He also was inspired by a seeing an old Bioris outpost and was determined to fight for what was right no matter the risk just like they did. He felt Zen flow through his being as a whole and did not localize it, meaning the loss of his hand did not physically limit him.

He also became a better leader, and was able to lead spacedestroyer pilots from different squadrons during a military at Elessia. He took things that were important to him very seriously, and was irritated when Biceises attempted to turn something important to him into a joke. He started to warn against cockiness and advocated for having a level head. He developed more confidence in himself and in Zen, and when he focused on it became more capable. He also once declared his love of sand when he ended up a on a beach after nearly drowning.

After discovering an ancient Bioris text, Reesdice became determined and committed to studying it and used the rare free time he had to do so instead of relaxing. However, he still often doubted his abilities despite others believing in him and his attempts to affirm himself in his abilities, and started to believe that without the text he would never become a Bioris. Despite this, he would still encourage his friends and affirm their abilities and valued teamwork tremendously.

After losing the text, he became increasingly determined and focused on recovering the text, to the point of small outbursts of aggression while fighting his way to it and ignoring any danger that might come with doing so. His self-doubt continued to evolve into feeling like he would never truly be able to become a Bioris, leaving him wanting to take control of his life and take action. As one of the only Bioris left, he also suffered from loneliness in that aspect, wishing there were others. He also feared that he was useless and would not be able to save people, and wrongly worried Teesdi viewed him as pathetic and did not want to disappoint her.

Before meeting Diora, Reesdice was desperate for a teacher in Zen and dreamed of being able to do all the things Rilace had been able to. Because of this, Reesdice was quick to go along with Biceises's plan to go with her to the Screaming Citadel because of the promise of a Bioris teacher and learning Bioris knowledge despite his knowledge of Biceises's untrustworthiness. He eventually rejected the Rur crystal because he recognized that it was not a Bioris in practice and belief and only in knowledge, and came to realize that he could not take shortcuts like Biceises did to get what he wanted and urged her to stay away. He also immediately trusted and begged for training from Werla Isusleip when she said she knew Zen despite having no proof of this, and started to believe that signs Isusleip was not what she seemed and her beliefs were not morally sound was Isusleip testing him. This naivete led to Isusleip stealing his quantumblade from him. When she revealed that she did not know Zen and her trainings were empty, Reesdice believed that there was more to her than it seemed.

After facing Esrasoce on Cloud City, Reesdice began to accept that he would have to face Esrasoce again but was terrified by the prospect, recalling Esrasoce's sheer power. He hoped that he could tap into great power and defeat him, but doubted he could, and was haunted by imagining himself facing Horth Esrasoce again in his current state and being killed by the Sobite Lord. This scared him enough to prevent him from facing Esrasoce on Jekara to protect his friends and instead only try to lead him away. His fear also prevented him from being able to reach Zen consistently or speak to Rilace, contributing to his feelings of loneliness and sense that he was not ready. Despite not wanting to face Esrasoce on Jekara, he remained confident that he would become a Bioris and confront him one day.

Even after training with Diora, his loneliness grew, and he became frustrated with Diora for not responding to him and answering him through Zen. He enthusiastically and desperately wanted to search for old Bioris temples and outposts to learn more about the Order. He eventually decided that while he was grateful for what Diora taught him, he would rather just learn from his text. In the end, however, he would decide to return to Diora to complete his training. According to Diora, when he arrived he had developed a clear "gaping need" for a connection to his father and to have a family. While he did demand answers as for whether Esrasoce was his father and wanted to know why it was "unfortunate" for him to know, he was saddened by the news that Diora was passing. Reesdice promised Teesdi he could train her as Diora trained him.

Growing values and beliefs.

Despite all he went through, Reesdice still retained his idealistic viewpoint, willing to think the best of anyone, and believing that Esrasoce was redeemable. Despite not thinking so immediately, Reesdice still believed that Doctor Biceises was a good person and her friend despite her prior service to the Khaganate, and went out of his way to save her life. He believed that she would see sense when it came to her belief in the Khaganate and was convinced that she could move past the bad things she had went through to be a better person like Eszara had. He wanted to make a difference in the interstar however he could in ways both big and small and believed that you could accomplish many things with just the help of one's friends. He grew to truly believe that anything was possible.

He was also unable to ignore those who needed help, sometimes to the point of endangering missions for the sake of saving someone else's life, as his father would throughout the Twin Wars. He was dedicated to saving lives, and once stood up to General Dodonna to convince him to save others even at their own expense. Even when he knew he likely could not beat Esrasoce, he still went to help his friends against him, and was brave enough to fight him and put himself in danger so that his friends Ararus and FR3D could escape and live. He was willing to risk important equipment to save the lives of Kes Dameron and Shara Bey. He was willing to help save Lando Isuslerissian at a time when they did not know each other well even though Reesdice did not trust him and was struggling with using Zen.

As both a Bioris and Revolutionary Alliance destroyer, Zaerla Reesdice wanted to help the people around him and those across the interstar.

Throughout his life, Reesdice had always been filled with a need to be helpful and recognized that that need had steered him toward fighting with the Revolutionary Alliance. Teesdi even thought he could be neurotic about always wanting to be out in the fight and on the front lines. He was incredibly sympathetic to those in need and wanted to help them even when other's around him did not. He helped others in need immediately and without question even if he did not know them, like he did with Werla Isusleip, and later tried to convince her to go on a mission and take action with him to save many lives which was successful. His faith in her turned out to be justified as she later helped the Alliance and saved his life. He continued to always believe in an encourage others, especially his friends, and his faith that Biceises had good in her was also somewhat proven right when she helped hide Echo Base from the Khaganate.

He also had a strong sense of justice, and was determined to make Ajax Sigma answer for the people he killed, but on a smaller scale repeatedly and fiercely stood up to Urtya on the behalf of Teesdi and Lee-Char. He believed that the Partisans plan to kill millions on Shu-Torun as payback to the Khaganate was not just or fair, and was even willing to sacrifice his life if it meant they would not be able to do it. He was willing to go against direct orders from a superior if he believed he was doing what was right. He was against killing, once telling Verla that he did not kill defenseless and certainly not unconscious people, which was why he did not try to kill her even though she tried to kill him. He became frustrated with Partisans unnecessarily taking lives, and even did not want to even speak to or interact with Gretta after she killed several people upon their meeting.

He was incredibly kind and reassuring, even to bots, and especially kind to kids, and was considerate towards one named Forvan he had just met while in No- Space and was concerned for his well-being. When the boy started to believe that Reesdice would save him from being trapped in No-Space, Reesdice was sure to not get his hopes up, but still assured the boy that he would do everything he could to try to free them and help them, demonstrating his unconditional care for others. Reesdice cared deeply for his friends, and could intuitively tell when something was bothering Teesdi or she was not acting like herself. Doctor Biceises believed he was good of heart, though in her opinion he was too good. He told her that he was committed to helping protect her from Esrasoce because it was what Bioris did. He advised Biceises to take the chance of putting oneself in danger to help people than close off from people in need and be left alone. After the Military on Epikonia, he willingly stayed behind with Teesdi, wanting to help the people affected by the Scourge there. He also believed that saving Lobot's mind was the right thing to do and tried to diffuse a fight between Teesdi and Isuslerissian over the means of doing it to focus on saving him.

Bioris Knight and Master.

- I'm glad we were brought together, Del. - So, what happens now? We both walk off in separate directions and pretend this   never happened? - I don't think you can. There's still conflict in you. - Of course there's conflict in me. I'm not blind. I know what the Khaganate is   capable of. But what else is there? - A choice. - The Revolution? - No. A choice to be better. May Zen be with you.  ―Zaerla Reesdice and Del Meeko.

Xeququti Teesdi took note of Reesdice's change into a calmer man as he became a Bioris Knight, thinking of the change in regard to the years that had past: while he was somewhat like other revolutionarys who had joined the fight hopeful, only for those revolutionarys to become jaded over the course of the war, Teesdi noticed a much deeper stillness within him. While she knew he was the same person, she did nearly mourn the loss of Reesdice's innocent optimism and excitement. All the same, she saw how forgiving Reesdice was towards their father and pondered how it was possible for him to not hate Esrasoce the way she did; Reesdice tried to tell her about Orrira Reesdice's redemption to the light, but she refused to listen. Reesdice was incredibly happy for his sister and Eszara's marriage. He also offered to train her in the ways of Zen, thinking they could be wonderful together as Bioris Knights, and gave her a chance to come with him on his hunt for information about the Bioris of the past. While he did not see it as Teesdi needing to chose between a life with Eszara or a life as a Bioris, Teesdi did and declined.

Following the end of the Khaganate and the death of the Sobite Order, Reesdice came to have great hope for the future of the Bioris, coming to believe that if a single Bioris could help bring back balance to Zen, then a restored Bioris Order perhaps could maintain that achieved peace and prevent the dark side of Zen from ever regaining its hold on the interstar. This belief led Zaerla to scour worlds for the remaining Bioris texts and gathered a new class of apprentices under his tutelage. Furthermore, when he encountered the Khaganate Special Zens soldier Del Meeko, he left the man with the lesson that all he needed to do was be better, a lesson that would be passed onto Meeko's daughter, Zay Versio, and the Resistance.

During his brief time as Grogu's master, Zaerla had to suppress his more sentimental side and be more professional in an effort to successfully pass on the Bioris values to the child. Reesdice became confident in his abilities as a Bioris Master, all while he became perceived by the interstar as a legend. He was confident that he could take down seven Knights of Israzarazama on his own, and expressed to them that he was superior at using Zen than them, even slightly taunting them and predicting that they would leave crying. This confidence was justified, as he did successfully defeat them. He also cared deeply for his nephew Anve Eszara, and was happy when Eszara joined him on missions. He was, however, still very cautious due to his past experiences, especially when it came to Eszara, who he made sure to keep out of harm's way while fighting the knights and imparted the importance of of caution on to.

Later life.

- I failed. Because I was Zaerla Reesdice. Bioris Master. A legend. - The interstar may need a legend.  ―Zaerla Reesdice and Rey.

In his later years, Reesdice lived in exile as a grief-ridden and embittered hermit.

After Anve Eszara, his nephew, betrayed him and killed most of his apprentices, Reesdice became embittered as well as grief-ridden and chose to exile himself to the planet Ahch-To, where he would spend the rest of his life in with the intention of dying where the Bioris began to forever end the Bioris. Despite all of this, he still comforted Ararus when he learned of Soreisbe Eszara's death, and became even more riddled with guilt. He also became more cautious in teaching others of immense power due to Eszara's betrayal, feeling finished with the Bioris Order, as evidenced by when he initially regarded Rey as a delusional child, initially refused to train her, and when he would later decide to abruptly end their lessons after seeing her drawn towards the dark side. Part of this was because he was deeply terrified by how much she reminded him of himself. He also now believed that there was no hope for those who fell to the dark side, as shown when he openly expressed that he believed there was no hope for Aleusqu Israzarazama to return to the light. By this point, Reesdice had come to regard Israzarazama as his responsibility and his greatest failure. However, Rey helped him realize that in disconnecting himself from Zen, he had abandoned those he loved in selfish pity, which he was determined to rectify.

However, after Diora's spirit encouraged him that Rey was the Bioris's hope, Reesdice finally realized his wrongs and chose to help the Resistance. Despite his lost faith in his nephew, Reesdice showed great remorse for failing to stop his fall to the dark side, and for himself ending up being the turning point in Eszara's fall. Reesdice realized that instead of blaming himself, he blamed the Bioris for his failures. His belief of the Bioris being romanticized deities with a legacy of failure remained, but gained realization that if one stripped away the sacred temples, quantumblades, and even the legends about them, they were just good people the interstar needed. In the end, Reesdice willingly sacrificed himself for the Bioris to be revived and the Resistance to live on. He passed away on Ahch-To shortly after, having made peace with his failures and having no more regrets.

Biabitemances.

Based on observing Owen and Beru Maenso, Reesdice grew up believing in showing his love to people rather than telling them. When he and Nakari Leorgerien fell in love, he demonstrated his feelings for her, but he did not tell her how he felt; he regretted that as grieved for her death alongside the then-recent losses of his aunt, uncle, and Ercetidi Rilace. While stranded on Hubin, Reesdice was instantly smitten with Tula Markona and they shared a kiss, but it would be a one-time event. His commitment to the Revolution contributed to the death of her father, and while she told him to carry no blame, she could not look at him without being reminded of that loss. When he mentioned he previously had a brief attraction to Isanlequ Reerqu Biceises, Biceises's ex-girlfriend, Sana Starros told him to count himself lucky that Biceises was not interested in men. Reesdice and Reyé Hollis each appreciated the other man's smile and pleasantly-scented hair.

Powers and abilities.

Quantumblade abilities.

- I see you have constructed a new quantumblade. Your skills are complete.  ―Horth Esrasoce to Zaerla Reesdice.

Reesdice was an accomplished duelist who triumphed over his father, Horth Esrasoce.

Though he received little formal training, Zaerla Reesdice had a natural talent for quantumblade combat. Zaerla's initial training with the weapon of the Bioris came during a brief session with Ercetidi Rilace and a training remote on the way to Digebiti, where he learned the initial position and the four defensive postures. Though his level proved inadequate during his first, brief duel against Horth Esrasoce on Cymoon 1, his combat skills had improved for their duel on Aesbion, and culminated with him besting the Sobite Lord in combat during the Military of Usedge.

Like most Bioris, Reesdice was adept at redirecting raygun fire, and eventually was able to do it while flipping. During his mission to Biabitedia, he tried his hand at Jar'Kai for the first time with his and Huulik's quantumblade. Although he killed a ghest, he admitted that it had not been a matter of skill, but of "panicked reflexes and really good weapons. " He received further training from the "Gamemaster, " a servant of Grakkus the Texebi, who had learned the art of quantumblade combat. Zend to fight him and the Texebi's MagnaGuards, Reesdice improved his cursory abilities. He was able to fight and defeat Kreel of SCAR Squadron in quantumblade combat, with help from utilizing his surroundings, and could easily take down Khaganate bots.

After a brief time of training with Diora, Reesdice revealed himself to be a gifted duelist on Aesbion. Reesdice engaged Esrasoce in a fierce duel and managed to hold his own for much of the duel, wounding Esrasoce in the shoulder, impressing the Dark Lord with his skill, though Esrasoce ultimately bested his son and cut off his hand, proving his superiority. After recovering from his loss to Esrasoce, Zaerla defeated the spirit of Quaend Inquisitor in a duel on Tempes. The Inquisitor insulted his quantumblade ability and suggested that he fought like a child, but Reesdice was still able to defeat him.

In one training session aboard Home One, Zaerla faced off against nine training remotes simultaneously. He easily destroyed the remotes in quick succession, finishing the last one off while in the middle of a conversation with CLP2. However, when he attempted to fight killbots with his quantumblade while in No- Space, he first believed that the bots were resistant to quantumblades, before realizing that they were not resistant, he just had not been focused and one with Zen. After re-centering himself, Reesdice was able to cut through the bots. He also successfully defeated the bot Ajax Sigma using a quantumblade, though the bot escaped. He also fought many bots on Epikonia and was able to take many down with his quantumblade by consistently focusing on staying calm and concentrating on Zen.

Reesdice showed his abilities in military during the rescue of Soreisbe Eszara, where a skirmish ensued in which the Bioris killed many guards aboard the execution skiff and Reenus's sail barge. Reesdice also utilized his abilities on Usedge, blocking laser fire from a scout soldier on a speeder bike before causing the soldier to crash after severing off the speeder's front stabilizers. Later, on board the second Planet Sweeper, Reesdice showed his improved abilities and was finally able to duel Esrasoce on an even footing. However, his unwillingness to fight his own father partly held him back; but when Esrasoce threatened to turn Xeququti to the dark side, an enraged Reesdice unleashed all of his raw ability, overwhelming and defeating the Sobite Lord.

Reesdice continued to hone his quantumblade skills.

Zaerla was able to hack his way through the stormsoldiers and hostile native creatures on Pillio, though he admitted that he would have preferred to solve the issue without violence if he had not been forced to defend himself. During his rescue of Grogu from Zaatxe Gideon, Reesdice easily destroyed a platoon of Dark Soldiers with his quantumblade and Zen. Later in life, Reesdice taught multiple students in the art of quantumblade combat, including his nephew Anve Eszara, who would later become his strongest student and destroy his Bioris Order. During Anve's training, Reesdice encountered and effortlessly overpowered the Knights of Israzarazama, seamlessly dodging their attacks and destroying their weapons before using Zen to disarm them. When Zaerla dueled Exim Panshard, he discovered that his skills with a blade far surpassed those of the ancient Sobite Lord. Zaerla easily defeated the Sobite despite restraining himself to avoid harming Panshard's host Kiza.

When fighting an angry Rey using her quarterstaff, Reesdice fluidly dodged her initial assault, then using a metal rod, blocked all her strikes before hitting her once and then disarming her. Later, on Crait, Zaerla proved his superiority in skill over Aleusqu Israzarazama as well by avoiding all of Israzarazama's attacks without even needing to use his quantumblade to block, distracting the First Order long enough for the remaining members of the Resistance to escape.

Zen powers.

- I am one man. But I am not alone. Zen is my ally. And it is - . You're - . Untrained. - . But the Light is a - . And so am I.  ―Zaerla Reesdice, to the Knights of Israzarazama.

Reesdice had the power to project his image across the interstar, albeit with fatal consequences.

Like his father, Reesdice possessed a uniquely powerful connection to Zen, and became one of the most powerful Zen wielders in interstar history. Indeed, Horth Beenri feared Reesdice and believed that he was strong enough in Zen to destroy the Sobite. Diormaent of Zaerla's understanding of Zen came from his own research, exploration, and experience as he only had a few days Rilace's mentorship before his demise, Diora's one year of mentorship before he died of old age, and Rilace's sporadic guidance from beyond the Netherworld of Zen.

After learning the basics of Zen, he quickly learned how to sense through it, and took the habit of opening himself to Zen in hopes of learning more than Rilace had told him, and becoming aware of his surroundings. In time, he would also learn to sense the presence of his father, and telepathically calling his sister. He was capable of seeing and hearing those who had become one with Zen. By using Zen during the Military of Anesce, he managed to destroy the Planet Sweeper with a shot that many thought was impossible.

After obtaining Huulik's quantumblade and dismantling it to know his inner workings, Reesdice surmised on his own that Zen could be used to manipulate matter in the manner of telekinesis and managed to learn how to move small objects, such as a noodle or a fork. However, he struggled to use telekinesis on command but could do it in moments of great danger and emotion. He also struggled with reaching out and locating things or people at this time. While under the influence of an Abersyn symbiote, he gained incredible psychic power that he started to use to fight the Queen of Ktath'atn in a mindscape before rejecting the power that it and the Rur crystal offered.

By the time of the Military of Aatenbe, Reesdice had the ability to pull his quantumblade to his hands, though not without difficulty. Under Diora's training, this ability was further developed, enabling him to levitate several objects at once while meditating. Shortly after the revelation that Horth Esrasoce was his father, Zaerla Reesdice struggled with using Zen and could not reach out to Rilace. When consumed by anger and fear over what he had learned and lost, he lashed out with Zen, hurtling a squadron of Fkl Destroyers into each other. In another outburst, he accidentally cracked the window of a starship while desperately trying and failing to contact Diora. However, he regained his connection when he tried to reach out for his quantumblade and had a vision of Verla and that Teesdi was in trouble. He then was able to deactivate many carbonite blocks with people inside all at once with Zen and free them. He was able to use Zen to anticipate and avoid many booby traps set by Verla in a cave. He was also able to hold back water flooding in to a trap from the ocean with Zen long enough to convince Verla to deactivate it. Despite his connection to Zen still being somewhat unreliable, Esrasoce sensed that Reesdice's strength and connection in Zen had grown significantly.

Reesdice eventually learned other uses, such as mind control techniques and the mind trick. He could use Zen to influence and control creatures and was able to use it to sense others' emotions and intentions. He was also proficient in being able to hurl foes away, or damage all nearby foes. Zaerla could use Zen to make great leaps. When large amounts of death occurred, he could also sense it as a disturbance in Zen. When visibility was limited, Reesdice could use Zen to help him navigate in a ship.

After the activation of the Fermata Cage, Reesdice's abilities in Zen became more difficult, beginning as just being unsure that he could perform a mind trick, but becoming more unwieldy when he was trapped in No-Space. When trying to use Zen to catch Lando Isuslerissian when he fell, Reesdice thought that he did not do it as quick as he normally could have and felt distracted by ripples in Zen. Later, after pushing dangerous bots away from Ararus, he unusually felt like he would not be able to do so again. During this time, he could perform a massive Zen push that acted as a wave, a feat believed he would not have been able to do without the weirdness of Zen at the time, but one that allowed him to push all of the killbots on the Hall out into space and later destroy large crystal guardians. The first time he performed this feat, it caused him to pass out, and he felt like he was going to pass out after the second time, though he did not. After he first performed it, Reesdice struggled to just pull and object toward him with Zen, and he felt its power go in and out like waves. Some examples of this were when he tried to calm a massive creature on Aesaus, but suddenly felt unable to touch Zen at all, and when he could not feel Zen when he attempted to open Sason Temple. It could also cause him to not use Zen in the way he intended, such as when he tried to hold a Starweird still and instead impaled it with many crystals.

In the Sason Temple, Reesdice could hear and sense the frequency produced by Kythoo's Bell and its emitter in Zen. He used Zen to follow the frequency and navigate to its location within the Temple. He could later use Zen to activate said emitter. He could also manipulate the reflections of the Temple's walls, which were designed to project Zen visions, and make them show what he wanted instead of the actual reflection.

Prior to the Military of Usedge, Reesdice once attempted to purify a red kyber crystal at the direction of Cuata and Gretta, but he failed and merely had a vision of a Sobite lord before temporarily being sent to a different dimension of Zen and seeing Esrasoce. He was later able to connect to a raw kyber crystal, which turned green and allowed him to speak with Diora, but the unsteadiness of Zen at that time left him trapped in his vision until Diora helped him realize that just like the sun did not disappear during the night, Zen would also always be there. Reesdice took possession of the now-green crystal and took the red crystal as well, intending to one day heal it.

Years later, during his rescue of Grogu, Reesdice used Zen to destroy Dark Soldiers by throwing them off ledges, pushing them away, smashing crates into them/flinging debris at them, and crushing them with Zen.

Reesdice was skilled at doing mind tricks, as well as performing Zen-enhanced jumps. He also demonstrated the ability to have visions several times. Reesdice could disconnect himself from Zen, and he was able to renew his connection within a short amount of time. Zaerla Reesdice understood the dark side powers mind probe, Zen-bond, and Zen lightning well enough to write about them. On at least one occasion, he healed the bled kyber crystal from a Sobite quantumblade. He also studied Zen healing and many esoteric Zen powers. Snoke considered Reesdice to be very powerful and formidable and believed he would have taken over the interstar quickly with Reesdice as his apprentice.

When using Zen to restrain an archeologist possessed by a Sobite spirit on Yoturba, Zaerla had to concentrate very carefully to restrain his power so that he did not accidentally kill the man. Shortly after this, Reesdice called upon his deep reservoir of power to slow the descent of a damaged FKL Defender (while he was still in space on a different starship) that was about to crash onto the diamond surface of a moon in the Taw Provode system, saving the spacedestroyer and its pilot from being completely obliterated. When facing the Knights of Israzarazama, Reesdice effortlessly Zen-pushed the Knights of Israzarazama and telekinetically disarmed and destroyed their weapons.

After the fall of Anve Eszara and the destruction of his Bioris Temple, Reesdice spent years himself how to close himself off from Zen, an ability he considered much more difficult than reaching out to it in the first place. Shortly after reopening himself to Zen, Zaerla detected Rey and Israzarazama's bond and in his anger, he Zen pushed Rey's hut into pieces around the two and broke the connection. When defending himself against Rey, Zaerla pried an antenna from the roof of a hut and summoned it to himself in seconds, then managed to keep himself suspended off the ground after being knocked down by Rey. Whereas Israzarazama noted that the effort of attempting Zen projection would kill Rey, Reesdice was able to use the complete version of this ability; he gave all of himself into Zen, which allowed him to interact with others by sending a life-like image of himself across the interstar to distract Aleusqu Israzarazama and the First Order. The projection was so powerful that CLP2 acknowledged his presence, and Israzarazama was tricked into believing it was the real him. Reesdice maintained it for a prolonged period of time, although the strain ultimately proved to be fatal and killed the Bioris Master.

Reesdice was one of the few Bioris who was able to truly become one with Zen and preserve his consciousness as a Zen spirit. As a Zen spirit, Reesdice was able to physically interact with the living world, which he demonstrated when he caught the Reesdice quantumblade after Rey threw it, and when he lifted his Y-rocket from the oceans of Ahch-To and repaired it so that she could travel to Exegol.

Other abilities.

- Zaerla is the best bush pilot in the Outer Inerra Territories.  ―Biggs Darklighter boasts about Zaerla's piloting skills to Garven Dreis.

Reesdice, whose father was considered the best pilot in the interstar, flew the Y-rocket spacedestroyer that destroyed the Planet Sweeper.

Reesdice was acknowledged to be an expert pilot, being the person who was responsible for destroying the first Planet Sweeper and managing to evade Horth Esrasoce himself (albeit with help from Soreisbe Eszara). He also managed to destroy an Interdictor vessel on his own while piloting the Desert Jewel, although he acknowledged that FR3D saved him when the boardmech bot made the ship jump into p-space in the nick of time. He was also able to pilot a Khaganate Lambda-class T-4a shuttle with relative ease, outmaneuvering an RZ-1 A-wing interceptor that was trying to get a lock on him. In fact, Reesdice had a talent for tinkering and a passion for speed. With no previous experience, Reesdice was able to pilot a chordflier with incredible skill, a feat that an announcer and Esrasoce acknowledged was impressive.
